output
1	# Milestone shape: interface, step spines, wiring, system
2	
3	Read when writing a milestone's tasks. Examples: `examples-phase0.md`.
4	
5	A milestone is planned from its pipelines' steps: each step has one owner, one module and typed `In → Out`, so it is the unit of work. The plan freezes every boundary between steps first, so the work on both sides starts at once instead of queueing behind one spine. Every task carries `**Kind:**`:
6	
7	```
8	## Phase 0: Interface              interface (solo, first)       types, signatures, samples, fakes, exports, __main__
9	## Phase 1: Step spines            spine, one per step           each writes its owner module only
10	## Phase 2: Wiring and thicken     wiring, one per pipeline      its entry module, smoke test
11	                                   thicken (tasks.md)            each after its step's spine
12	## Phase 3: System                 system (solo, last)           fakes swapped for real providers
13	```
14	
15	Phases name the usual waves; the dependency graph (`tasks.md` step 9) decides what may start. Tester halves of split tasks start right after the interface.
16	
17	**a. Interface task** (`**Kind:** interface`, `**Step:** contract`, solo, `**Depends on:** —`). It freezes, as code, every boundary in the milestone whose two sides belong to different tasks:
18	- each contract `type` section the milestone's steps pass (`In → Out`), written into its owner's `types.py`, with a schema (pandera for a frame, JSON Schema across runtimes) and one sample per case in `tests/contracts/<component>.<Type>/`. A case naming a probe sample (`PB-01 samples/sync_added.json#0`) puts `PB-01:samples` in `Read:`; the task derives the type's sample from it, never copies it
19	- each new step's signature in its owner module, with the body `raise NotImplementedError("<task id>")` naming the task that replaces it
20	- for each function a pipeline calls from another pipeline or package (its `**Boundary:**` line): a fixture-backed fake serving its fixtures, where the caller's tests load it. A `call` edge to a contract symbol is mitigated by that fake; `none` there needs a reason (`dt lint`)
21	- for each object with behavior a step passes (a Protocol or class: `core.Dataset`, `runstore.RunWriter`) and each call into another component's function: the Protocol or signature plus a fixture-backed replay double in `tests/contracts/<component>.<Type>/`. The double is that type's sample, not a fake: the system task never swaps it
22	- each component's `__init__.py` exports, and the package's `__main__.py`: the dispatch from each pipeline's trigger to its entry function, whose signature it stubs naming the wiring task, in that pipeline's own entry module
23	
24	Not frozen: helpers, private functions, anything one task owns end to end, and boundaries an earlier milestone froze. `Read:` the type and boundary symbols. Acceptance: every sample validates against its type; every signature imports; each fake and replay double serves its fixtures. `Writes:` each component folder as one glob (`src/gridload/profile/**`) plus its contract folders; `task-size` doesn't count them. A milestone across packages may have one interface task per provider, all solo and first; interface tasks never depend on each other. When one runtime's types are generated from another's schema, the providing interface task runs the generator and writes the generated file too (its `Writes:` claims it).
25	
26	**b. Step spines** (`**Kind:** spine`, `**Step:** PL-NN.<function>`, solo, XS or S). One per new step, each depending only on the interface task: it replaces the stub naming it with the step's spine version from the step table. `Writes:` its owner module and that module's unit test, nothing else; steps sharing one module share one spine task. Acceptance: the step's input sample gives a schema-valid output; the signature is unchanged. A step another pipeline provides (`PL-01 as built`) gets no spine.
27	
28	**c. Wiring** (`**Kind:** wiring`, `**Step:** PL-NN`, solo). One per new pipeline: the entry function in the pipeline's own entry module (the layout's, never one module shared by several pipelines), so wiring tasks run side by side, and its smoke test. `__main__.py` is the interface task's. It depends on its pipeline's spines, since the smoke runs them (`order`, `none: integration: …`), never on their thicken tasks. A call to another pipeline or package runs against that boundary's fake, so wiring PL-02 never waits for PL-01. Acceptance: the smoke command runs end to end and is committed as the pipeline's smoke test; the pipeline's pre-build sections are replaced by a `dt layout` pointer (`dt land` runs `dt prebuild-clear` when the wiring lands).
29	
30	**d. System task** (`**Kind:** system`, solo, last). It swaps each fake for the real provider, in the entry its wiring wrote (a `file` edge), and runs every smoke command and the milestone's golden cases on the real chain; the boundary samples still validate against real output. Multi-package: one per new system pipeline, `**Step:**` that pipeline, `**Package:**` the owner of its first step, `**Reason:** system task`; the root has no pre-build sections, so no `dt layout` bullet. Single package: `**Step:**` the calling pipeline. A milestone with no fake has no system task.
31	
32	**Folds** keep the task count down. The pipeline's wiring depends on a fold task, so a fold is allowed only where that task is XS and the critical path doesn't grow. Record each on its task as `**Reason:** fold: <rule>`:
33	- A step with a golden case whose spine and thicken are both in the milestone, or whose spine version is its whole behavior, may be one split thicken task that replaces the stub itself; with a thicken folded in, its acceptance keeps the baseline selectable.
34	- A trivial step (pass-through, print) is built by its pipeline's wiring task; its stub names the wiring task.
35	
36	**Fakes and stubs.** A fake is allowed only where a pipeline calls another pipeline or package: both sides test against the same fixtures, so neither drifts from the contract unseen. Mocking a step is forbidden: a step's spine is the real baseline, and its consumer builds against the type's sample (for a Protocol or class, its replay double). The interface stubs are the floor's one stub carve-out (`build-setup`'s CR-02): allowed while the task they name is open; the gate fails on one naming a done task, the milestone exit on any.
37	
38	**Frozen means frozen.** Changing a frozen type, sample, signature or fake is a `contract-change`: `architecture-design` Revise, then a new interface task naming the symbol in `Read:`, never an edit to make a test pass. The bar enforces it (CR-09): once a contract folder exists at a task's base the implementer can't write it, and `dt guard` asks before any edit no open interface task covers. The re-freeze is that interface task, run by an implementer agent, never the main session: its `Writes:` names the frozen folders it changes, which opens them to it alone.
39

1	# Example: interface-first M1 (gridload, single package)
2	
3	PL-01 Load meter readings (`read_raw` → `apply_registry`, owner `data`) and PL-02 Seasonality report (`load_readings` as PL-01 built, then `fit_seasonality` → `spectrum` → `deseason` → `write_report`, owner `profile`). Golden cases TC-01 (registry), TC-02 (flat spectrum), TC-03 (planted sine). The contract has a `type` section for every `In → Out`: `data.RawReadings`, `data.Readings`, `profile.Load`, `profile.Profile`, `profile.Spectrum`, `profile.SeasonalityReport`; `data.load_readings` carries a `**Boundary:**` line because PL-02 calls it.
4	
5	```
6	wave 0  T01  interface
7	wave 1  spines T02 read_raw · T03 apply_registry · T04 fit_seasonality · T05 spectrum · T06 deseason · T07 write_report
8	        testers of T10 registry · T11 LS amplitudes · T12 Fourier seasonality · T13 seasonal removal · T14 system
9	wave 2  wiring T08 PL-01 · T09 PL-02 (on the load_readings fake); implementers of T10 ← T03 · T11 ← T05 · T12 ← T04 · T13 ← T06
10	wave 3  T14  system: PL-02 on the real PL-01
11	```
12	
13	14 tasks, critical path 4 (T01 → T05 → T11 → T14, or any other four-task chain), 11 agents in wave 1, every spine and thicken XS or S. Checkpoints after T01 (barrier), T07 and T13, and the milestone exit after T14. No fold: each step's spine is a real baseline worth testing alone. The system task names TC cases, so it is split like the thicken tasks.

62	T11's tester starts in wave 1: it tests `spectrum` as T01 declares it and fails on `NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`. Its implementer follows its tester and T05, nothing else. Wiring T09 waits on PL-02's spines because its smoke runs them, but not on PL-01: the fake stands in.
63	
64	The graph, excerpted (one row per `Depends on` other than on T01, 16, plus the mitigated edges):
65	
66	```markdown
67	## Dependency graph
68	| From | To | Kind | Mitigated by |
69	|---|---|---|---|
70	| M1-T05 spectrum spine | M1-T04 fit_seasonality spine | data | profile.Profile sample (M1-T01) |
71	| M1-T09 wire PL-02 | M1-T05 spectrum spine | order | none: integration: the smoke runs it |
72	| M1-T09 wire PL-02 | M1-T08 wire PL-01 | call | data.load_readings fake (M1-T01) |
73	| M1-T11 LS amplitudes | M1-T05 spectrum spine | code | none: thickens the same module |
74	| M1-T11 LS amplitudes | M1-T12 Fourier seasonality | data | profile.Profile sample (M1-T01): the spectrum takes any Profile |
75	| M1-T14 system | M1-T09 wire PL-02 | file | none: swaps the fake in the entry M1-T09 wrote |
76	```
77	
78	**Multi-package.** The same shape across packages: the interface task freezes each `**Boundary:**` symbol in its `**Fixtures:**` folder (one interface task per provider when one would pass M), spines run in every package at once, a consumer's wiring runs on the provider's fake, and the system task (one per system pipeline, `**Package:**` the owner of its first step, `**Reason:** system task`, `--pkg` on the gate) swaps in the real providers.

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/src/rllab/core/types.py
"""The core's data types: teams, actions, steps, transitions and run settings (AD-09, AD-13)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

SOLO: str = "agents"

EnvName = Literal["snake", "corridor", "tank", "predprey"]
ViewName = Literal["compact", "grid", "index", "full", "limited"]
AgentName = Literal["tabular", "dqn", "reinforce", "ppo", "random", "scripted", "reference"]
OpponentMode = Literal["learn", "scripted", "random"]
SelfPlay = Literal["none", "latest", "pool"]
Ablation = Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target", "no_explore"]

_VIEWS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "snake": ("compact", "grid"),
    "corridor": ("index",),
    "tank": ("full", "limited"),
    "predprey": ("limited",),
}
_AGENT_ENVS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "tabular": ("snake", "corridor"),
    "dqn": ("snake", "corridor"),
    "reinforce": ("tank",),
    "ppo": ("tank", "predprey"),
    "reference": ("snake", "tank", "predprey"),
}
_ABLATION_AGENT = {"no_memory": "dqn", "no_target": "dqn", "no_explore": "tabular"}
_PREDPREY_TEAMS = frozenset({"predator", "prey"})
_MAX_SEED = 1_000_000
_MAX_FISH = 16
_GENERATIONS = 10


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ActionSpace:
    """Discrete (`n` actions) or continuous (`dim`-vector, clipped by the environment)."""

    kind: Literal["discrete", "continuous"]
    n: int = 0
    dim: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.kind == "discrete" and (self.n < 2 or self.dim != 0):
            raise ValueError(f"discrete actions need n >= 2 and dim == 0, got {self}")
        if self.kind == "continuous" and (self.dim < 1 or self.n != 0):
            raise ValueError(f"continuous actions need dim >= 1 and n == 0, got {self}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    """One team: its agent count, per-agent observation shape and shared action space."""

    n_agents: int
    obs_shape: tuple[int, ...]
    actions: ActionSpace

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.n_agents < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"n_agents must be >= 1, got {self.n_agents}")
        if not self.obs_shape or min(self.obs_shape) < 1:
            raise ValueError(f"obs_shape must be non-empty and positive, got {self.obs_shape}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StepResult:
    """One environment step, every map keyed by team and batch-first over that team's agents."""

    obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    reward: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    done: dict[str, np.ndarray]
    terminated: bool
    truncated: bool
    info: dict[str, float]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.obs.keys() == self.reward.keys() == self.done.keys():
            raise ValueError("obs, reward and done must name the same teams")
        if self.terminated and self.truncated:
            raise ValueError("a step cannot be both terminated and truncated")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Transition:
    """One learning team's step, every array batch-first over the team's agents."""

    obs: np.ndarray
    actions: np.ndarray
    reward: np.ndarray
    next_obs: np.ndarray
    terminated: np.ndarray
    active: np.ndarray
    truncated: bool

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        n = self.obs.shape[0]
        arrays = (self.actions, self.reward, self.next_obs, self.terminated, self.active)
        if any(a.shape[0] != n for a in arrays):
            raise ValueError("every Transition array must have the same number of agents")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rewards:
    """Reward sizes: food (or the corridor goal), death (or a catch) and every step."""

    food: float = 1.0
    death: float = -1.0
    step: float = 0.0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Exploration:
    """A linear decay from `start` to `end` over `decay_steps`, then flat (AGENT-02)."""

    start: float = 1.0
    end: float = 0.05
    decay_steps: int = 50_000

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 0 <= self.end <= self.start <= 1:
            raise ValueError(f"exploration needs 0 <= end <= start <= 1, got {self}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentParams:
    """Learner hyperparameters; each agent reads only its own (AD-11)."""

    lr: float
    gamma: float
    batch_size: int = 64
    memory_size: int = 50_000
    target_every: int = 1_000
    learn_start: int = 1_000
    hidden: int = 128
    net: Literal["mlp", "cnn"] = "mlp"
    rollout_steps: int = 2048
    epochs: int = 10
    clip: float = 0.2
    gae_lambda: float = 0.95
    games_per_update: int = 10

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 0 < self.gamma <= 1:
            raise ValueError(f"gamma must be in (0, 1], got {self.gamma}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunSettings:
    """A fully resolved run: the environment, the learner, its opposition and its budget."""

    name: str
    env: EnvName
    view: ViewName
    agent: AgentName
    n_agents: int
    opponents: Mapping[str, OpponentMode]
    self_play: SelfPlay
    pool_latest: float
    generations: int
    ablation: Ablation
    rewards: Rewards
    exploration: Exploration
    params: AgentParams
    steps: int
    seed: int
    eval_every: int
    eval_games: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _check_budget(self)
        _check_env(self)
        _check_opposition(self)


def _check_budget(s: RunSettings) -> None:
    if not 0 <= s.seed < _MAX_SEED:
        raise ValueError(f"seed must be in [0, {_MAX_SEED}), got {s.seed}")
    if s.steps <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f"steps must be > 0, got {s.steps}")


def _check_env(s: RunSettings) -> None:
    if s.view not in _VIEWS[s.env]:
        raise ValueError(f"view {s.view!r} does not belong to env {s.env!r}")
    if s.agent in _AGENT_ENVS and s.env not in _AGENT_ENVS[s.agent]:
        raise ValueError(f"agent {s.agent!r} does not run on env {s.env!r}")
    if s.ablation != "none" and _ABLATION_AGENT[s.ablation] != s.agent:
        raise ValueError(f"ablation {s.ablation!r} does not match agent {s.agent!r}")
    max_agents = _MAX_FISH if s.env == "tank" else 1
    if not 1 <= s.n_agents <= max_agents:
        raise ValueError(f"n_agents must be in [1, {max_agents}] for {s.env!r}, got {s.n_agents}")
    if s.env == "tank" and s.agent == "reference" and (s.n_agents != 1 or s.view != "full"):
        raise ValueError("agent 'reference' on the tank needs n_agents == 1 and the full view")


def _check_opposition(s: RunSettings) -> None:
    if s.env != "predprey":
        if s.opponents or s.self_play != "none":
            raise ValueError("opponents must be {} and self_play 'none' for single-team runs")
        return
    if set(s.opponents) != _PREDPREY_TEAMS:
        raise ValueError(f"opponents must name 'predator' and 'prey', got {sorted(s.opponents)}")
    learners = [team for team, mode in s.opponents.items() if mode == "learn"]
    if (s.self_play != "none") != (len(learners) == len(_PREDPREY_TEAMS)):
        raise ValueError(f"self_play {s.self_play!r} needs both teams to learn, and only then")
    if s.generations != (_GENERATIONS if learners else 0):
        raise ValueError(f"generations must be {_GENERATIONS} when a team learns, else 0")

"""The two behaviours every pipeline passes around: an environment and an agent (AD-03, AD-09)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol

import numpy as np

from rllab.core.types import StepResult, TeamSpec, Transition


class Env(Protocol):
    """A batch-first environment over named teams (AD-09, AD-13)."""

    teams: Mapping[str, TeamSpec]
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]: ...

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult: ...

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray: ...


class Agent(Protocol):
    """A player for one team: one action per agent of the team's batch."""

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray: ...

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None: ...

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None: ...

    """The same settings with only the seed replaced."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T02")

"""Snake on a square grid, one team of one agent (SNAKE-01-SNAKE-09)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Env, Rewards, StepResult


class Snake(Env):
    """The Snake game with the compact or the full-grid view."""

    def __init__(
        self, *, view: Literal["compact", "grid"], rewards: Rewards = Rewards(), size: int = 10
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def reset_to(
        self,
        body: Sequence[tuple[int, int]],
        heading: Literal["up", "right", "down", "left"],
        food: tuple[int, int],
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        """Start a game from a given board: cells are (row, col), the body head first."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T13")

"""The random baseline: a uniform action from a seeded generator (BASE-01)."""

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, Transition


class RandomPlayer(Agent):
    """Uniform over `n_actions` whatever `explore` says; never learns."""

    def __init__(self, n_actions: int, *, seed: int) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T16")

"""The scripted baseline: toward food if safe, else any safe move, else straight (BASE-02)."""

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, Transition


class ScriptedPlayer(Agent):
    """Deterministic rules on the compact view; ties go straight, then left, then right."""

        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T17")

"""The table-based value learner (AGENT-01, AGENT-02, ABL-03)."""

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, Transition


class TabularAgent(Agent):
    """One value per (observation, action), keyed on the observation's bytes."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        n_actions: int,
        params: AgentParams,
        exploration: Exploration,
        *,
        seed: int,
        explore_enabled: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T15")

"""The neural value learner: memory, target network, Huber loss (AGENT-03-AGENT-05, AD-05)."""

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, AgentParams, Exploration, TeamSpec, Transition


class DQNAgent(Agent):
    """A single-agent DQN sized from its team's observation shape and action count (AD-18)."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        team: TeamSpec,
        params: AgentParams,
        exploration: Exploration,
        *,
        seed: int,
        ablation: Literal["none", "no_memory", "no_target"] = "none",
    ) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")

U08.S51Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/src/rllab/runs/types.py (65 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/src/rllab/runs/types.py
"""What a run folder holds: its curve, its recorded games and the run record (AD-06)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import RunSettings

_GRID_FRAME_DIMS = 3  # T x H x W cell codes
_TANK_FRAME_DIMS = 3  # T x K x 4 circles
_CIRCLE_FIELDS = 4  # x, y, radius, kind


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurvePoint:
    """One greedy evaluation during training."""

    step: int
    games: int
    mean_score: float
    elapsed_s: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GameRecording:
    """One game: the start frame plus one frame per step, the rewards and every team's score."""

    seed: int
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
    frames: np.ndarray
    rewards: np.ndarray
    scores: dict[str, np.ndarray]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        n_frames = self.frames.shape[0]
        if self.frames.ndim != _GRID_FRAME_DIMS:
            raise ValueError(f"frames must be 3-D, got shape {self.frames.shape}")
        if self.board == "tank" and self.frames.shape[2] != _CIRCLE_FIELDS:
            raise ValueError(f"tank frames must be T x K x 4, got shape {self.frames.shape}")
        if self.rewards.shape != (n_frames - 1,):
            raise ValueError(f"rewards must have {n_frames - 1} entries, got {self.rewards.shape}")
        if any(s.shape != (n_frames,) for s in self.scores.values()):
            raise ValueError(f"each team's scores must have {n_frames} entries")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    """A run folder as read back: settings, curve, status, checkpoints, generations, games."""

    path: Path
    settings: RunSettings
    curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]
    status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]
    checkpoints: dict[str, Path]
    generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        steps = [p.step for p in self.curve]
        if any(b <= a for a, b in zip(steps, steps[1:], strict=False)):
            raise ValueError(f"curve steps must strictly increase, got {steps}")
        if self.status == "complete" and not self.checkpoints:
            raise ValueError("a complete run needs a checkpoint per learning team")

"""What a run folder holds: its curve, its recorded games and the run record (AD-06)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from itertools import pairwise
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import RunSettings

_FRAME_DIMS = 3  # T x H x W cell codes, or T x K x 4 circles
_CIRCLE_FIELDS = 4  # x, y, radius, kind


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurvePoint:
    """One greedy evaluation during training."""

    step: int
    games: int
    mean_score: float
    elapsed_s: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GameRecording:
    """One game: the start frame plus one frame per step, the rewards and every team's score."""

    seed: int
    board: Literal["grid", "tank"]
    frames: np.ndarray
    rewards: np.ndarray
    scores: dict[str, np.ndarray]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        n_frames = self.frames.shape[0]
        if self.frames.ndim != _FRAME_DIMS:
            raise ValueError(f"frames must be 3-D, got shape {self.frames.shape}")
        if self.board == "tank" and self.frames.shape[2] != _CIRCLE_FIELDS:
            raise ValueError(f"tank frames must be T x K x 4, got shape {self.frames.shape}")
        if self.rewards.shape != (n_frames - 1,):
            raise ValueError(f"rewards must have {n_frames - 1} entries, got {self.rewards.shape}")
        if any(s.shape != (n_frames,) for s in self.scores.values()):
            raise ValueError(f"each team's scores must have {n_frames} entries")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    """A run folder as read back: settings, curve, status, checkpoints, generations, games."""

    path: Path
    settings: RunSettings
    curve: tuple[CurvePoint, ...]
    status: Literal["running", "complete", "incomplete"]
    checkpoints: dict[str, Path]
    generations: dict[str, tuple[Path, ...]]
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        steps = [p.step for p in self.curve]
        if any(b <= a for a, b in pairwise(steps)):
            raise ValueError(f"curve steps must strictly increase, got {steps}")
        if self.status == "complete" and not self.checkpoints:
            raise ValueError("a complete run needs a checkpoint per learning team")

"""One folder per run: settings.json, curve.csv, checkpoints, games.npz, status (AD-06)."""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from rllab.core import SOLO, Agent, RunSettings
from rllab.runs.types import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord


class RunWriter:
    """Writes one run folder as the run goes; each curve point is flushed at once."""

    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = SOLO) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")


def create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> RunWriter:
    """Start a uniquely named run folder holding settings.json and status=running."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")


def open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord:
    """Read a run folder back; FileNotFoundError naming the run if it has no settings.json."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T03")

"""Evaluation results and options (AD-17)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO
from rllab.runs import GameRecording

_MAX_GAMES = 1000  # eval_seeds' limit


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScoreSet:
    """One player's held-out scores for one training seed, plus the games it recorded."""

    player: str
    train_seed: int
    scores: np.ndarray
    games: tuple[GameRecording, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.scores.ndim != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"scores must be 1-D, got shape {self.scores.shape}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalOptions:
    """Which team to score, how many games, and how many of them to record."""

    team: str = SOLO
    n_games: int = 200
    record: int = 0

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not 1 <= self.n_games <= _MAX_GAMES:
            raise ValueError(f"n_games must be in [1, {_MAX_GAMES}], got {self.n_games}")
        if not 0 <= self.record <= self.n_games:
            raise ValueError(f"record must be in [0, n_games], got {self.record}")

"""Claims to test and their results (EVAL-02-EVAL-05, REF-02)."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ClaimSpec:
    """`a` compared with `b`: greater, or not worse; `min_ratio` adds a mean-ratio check."""

    name: str
    a: str
    b: str
    direction: Literal["greater", "not_worse"]
    stretch: bool = False
    min_ratio: float | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Claim:
    """A tested claim: both means, raw and corrected p-values, the verdict and the method."""

    spec: ClaimSpec
    mean_a: float
    mean_b: float
    p_value: float
    p_corrected: float
    passed: bool
    method: str

"""What a report names: its players, their roles on the charts, and the runs it plots."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import SOLO
from rllab.runs import RunRecord

_BASELINES = ("random", "scripted")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlayerTarget:
    """A player: its run folders (one per training seed) or a named baseline."""

    runs: tuple[Path, ...] = ()
    baseline: str | None = None
    team: str = SOLO
    vs: tuple[str, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if bool(self.runs) == (self.baseline is not None):
            raise ValueError("a player names exactly one of runs and baseline")
        if self.baseline is not None and self.baseline not in _BASELINES:
            raise ValueError(f"baseline must be one of {_BASELINES}, got {self.baseline!r}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReportTargets:
    """The report's players and, in legend order, its baselines, ladder and ablations."""

    players: Mapping[str, PlayerTarget]
    baselines: tuple[str, ...]
    ladder: tuple[str, ...]
    ablations: tuple[str, ...]
    crossplay: tuple[Path, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        unknown = sorted(set(self.baselines + self.ladder + self.ablations) - set(self.players))
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"names not among the players: {unknown}")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CurveRuns:
    """The ladder and ablation groups, one RunRecord per training seed."""

    ladder: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]
    ablations: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]]

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/src/rllab/lab/cli.py
"""The command line: one entry call per seed or target, bad input returns 2."""

import argparse
import os
import sys
from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core import SOLO, load_settings
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl01_train import run_train
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl02_train_reference import run_train_reference
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl03_evaluate import run_evaluate
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl04_report import run_report
from rllab.lab.pipelines.pl05_replay import run_replay

_BAD_INPUT = 2


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Parse `argv` and run its subcommand; 0 on success, 2 on bad input."""
    try:
        args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
    except SystemExit as exc:
        return 0 if exc.code == 0 else _BAD_INPUT
    if args.smoke:
        os.environ["MPLBACKEND"] = "Agg"  # headless: the committed smoke tests open no window
    try:
        _dispatch(args)
    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
        print(exc, file=sys.stderr)
        return _BAD_INPUT
    return 0


def _dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    match args.command:
        case "train":
            reference = load_settings(args.settings).agent == "reference"
            for seed in args.seeds:
                if reference:
                    run_train_reference(args.settings, seed)
                else:
                    run_train(args.settings, seed)
        case "eval":
            for target in args.targets:
                run_evaluate(target, args.seed, env=args.env, team=args.team, vs=args.vs)
        case "report":
            run_report(args.spec, args.spec.parent / args.spec.stem)
        case "replay":
            pair = tuple(args.pair) if args.pair else None
            new_seed = args.seed if args.new else None
            run_replay(args.run, args.game, new_seed, (pair[0], pair[1]) if pair else None)


def _seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """`0-4` for seeds 0 to 4 inclusive, or one seed."""
    first, _, last = text.partition("-")
    return list(range(int(first), int(last or first) + 1))


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m rllab.lab")
    parser.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="headless, for the smoke tests")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    train = sub.add_parser("train", help="train a run per seed (PL-01, or PL-02 for reference)")
    train.add_argument("settings", type=Path)
    train.add_argument("--seeds", type=_seeds, default=[0])

    evaluate = sub.add_parser("eval", help="evaluate players on held-out seeds (PL-03)")
    evaluate.add_argument("targets", nargs="+", help="run folders, 'random' or 'scripted'")
    evaluate.add_argument("--env", choices=("snake", "tank", "predprey"), default="snake")
    evaluate.add_argument("--team", default=SOLO)
    evaluate.add_argument("--vs", default="scripted")
    evaluate.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)

    report = sub.add_parser("report", help="test the claims and draw the charts (PL-04)")
    report.add_argument("spec", type=Path)

    replay = sub.add_parser("replay", help="watch a game of a run (PL-05)")
    replay.add_argument("run", type=Path)
    which = replay.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    which.add_argument("--game", type=int, default=0)
    which.add_argument("--new", action="store_true", help="play a new game on --seed")
    which.add_argument("--pair", type=int, nargs=2, metavar=("PREDATOR_GEN", "PREY_GEN"))
    replay.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)

    for command in (train, evaluate, report, replay):
        command.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="headless, for the smoke tests")
    return parser

U08.S100Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/tests/contracts/kit.py (376 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/tests/contracts/kit.py
"""The contract kit: samples, schemas, fakes and replay doubles of the frozen interface (CR-09).

Each `tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/` folder holds `schema.json` and `samples/<case>.json`;
a function's folder also holds `fake.py`. Folder names hold dots, so load this kit by path:

    import importlib.util, sys
    from pathlib import Path

    KIT_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "contracts" / "kit.py"  # from tests/unit/rllab
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("contract_kit", KIT_PATH)
    kit = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules["contract_kit"] = kit
    spec.loader.exec_module(kit)

Then `kit.sample("core.RunSettings", "dqn_compact", RunSettings)` is a decoded sample,
`kit.fake("evaluate.evaluate").evaluate` the fixture-backed fake, and
`kit.env_double("core.Env", "corridor_goal")` a replay double of a recorded game.

Arrays are stored as `{"dtype": ..., "shape": [...], "data": [...]}`.
"""

import dataclasses
import importlib.util
import json
import sys
import types
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Union, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Agent, StepResult, TeamSpec, Transition
from rllab.runs import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord

CONTRACTS = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------- folders and samples
def folder(name: str) -> Path:
    """The contract folder of `name`, e.g. `core.RunSettings`."""
    path = CONTRACTS / name
    if not path.is_dir():
        raise LookupError(f"no contract folder {name}")
    return path


def raw_samples(name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Every JSON sample of `name`, by case name."""
    files = sorted((folder(name) / "samples").glob("*.json"))
    return {f.stem: json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) for f in files}


def raw_sample(name: str, case: str) -> Any:
    return json.loads((folder(name) / "samples" / f"{case}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def sample(name: str, case: str, tp: Any) -> Any:
    """Sample `case` of `name`, decoded into `tp`."""
    return decode(tp, raw_sample(name, case))


def schema(name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return json.loads((folder(name) / "schema.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def fake(name: str) -> types.ModuleType:
    """The fixture-backed fake module of the function `name`."""
    path = folder(name) / "fake.py"
    module_name = f"contract_fake_{name.replace('.', '_')}"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(module_name, path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise LookupError(f"no fake for {name}")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[module_name] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------ decoding and encoding
def decode(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    """`data` (parsed JSON) as an instance of the type `tp`; dataclass defaults fill gaps."""
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if tp is Any:
        return data
    if tp is np.ndarray:
        return np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=data["dtype"]).reshape(data["shape"])
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp) and isinstance(tp, type):
        hints = get_type_hints(tp)
        return tp(**{key: decode(hints[key], value) for key, value in data.items()})
    if origin is Literal:
        if data not in args:
            raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not one of {args}")
        return data
    if origin in {Union, types.UnionType}:
        if data is None and type(None) in args:
            return None
        (inner,) = [a for a in args if a is not type(None)]
        return decode(inner, data)
    if origin is tuple:
        if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
            return tuple(decode(args[0], x) for x in data)
        return tuple(decode(a, x) for a, x in zip(args, data, strict=True))
    if origin in {list, Sequence}:
        return [decode(args[0], x) for x in data]
    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
        return {key: decode(args[1], value) for key, value in data.items()}
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(data)
    if tp is float and isinstance(data, int | float) and not isinstance(data, bool):
        return float(data)
    if tp in {int, str, bool} and type(data) is tp:
        return data
    raise TypeError(f"cannot decode {data!r} as {tp}")


def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
    """The JSON form `decode` reads back."""
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        return {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return value.as_posix()
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(k): encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list | tuple):
        return [encode(v) for v in value]
    return value


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- schema validation
_JSON_TYPES: dict[str, tuple[type, ...]] = {
    "object": (dict,),
    "array": (list,),
    "string": (str,),
    "integer": (int,),
    "number": (int, float),
    "boolean": (bool,),
    "null": (type(None),),
}


def validate(schema_: dict[str, Any], data: Any) -> list[str]:
    """Every way `data` breaks `schema_` (a JSON Schema subset: $ref, type, enum, const,
    properties, required, additionalProperties, items, prefixItems, min/maxItems,
    minimum/maximum, anyOf). Empty when valid."""
    return _check(schema_, data, schema_, "$")


def _is_type(data: Any, name: str) -> bool:
    if isinstance(data, bool) and name in {"integer", "number"}:
        return False
    return isinstance(data, _JSON_TYPES[name])


def _check(s: dict[str, Any], data: Any, root: dict[str, Any], at: str) -> list[str]:
    if "$ref" in s:
        target: Any = root
        for part in s["$ref"].removeprefix("#/").split("/"):
            target = target[part]
        return _check(target, data, root, at)
    if "anyOf" in s:
        if any(not _check(option, data, root, at) for option in s["anyOf"]):
            return []
        return [f"{at}: matches none of anyOf"]
    errors: list[str] = []
    if "type" in s:
        names = s["type"] if isinstance(s["type"], list) else [s["type"]]
        if not any(_is_type(data, n) for n in names):
            return [f"{at}: expected {s['type']}, got {type(data).__name__}"]
    if "enum" in s and data not in s["enum"]:
        errors.append(f"{at}: {data!r} not in {s['enum']}")
    if "const" in s and data != s["const"]:
        errors.append(f"{at}: {data!r} != {s['const']!r}")
    if "minimum" in s and isinstance(data, int | float) and data < s["minimum"]:
        errors.append(f"{at}: {data} < {s['minimum']}")
    if "maximum" in s and isinstance(data, int | float) and data > s["maximum"]:
        errors.append(f"{at}: {data} > {s['maximum']}")
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        errors += _check_object(s, data, root, at)
    if isinstance(data, list):
        errors += _check_array(s, data, root, at)
    return errors


def _check_object(s: dict[str, Any], data: dict[str, Any], root: dict[str, Any], at: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = [f"{at}: missing {key!r}" for key in s.get("required", []) if key not in data]
    props = s.get("properties", {})
    extra = s.get("additionalProperties", True)
    for key, value in data.items():
        if key in props:
            errors += _check(props[key], value, root, f"{at}.{key}")
        elif extra is False:
            errors.append(f"{at}: unexpected {key!r}")
        elif isinstance(extra, dict):
            errors += _check(extra, value, root, f"{at}.{key}")
    return errors


def _check_array(s: dict[str, Any], data: list[Any], root: dict[str, Any], at: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    if len(data) < s.get("minItems", 0):
        errors.append(f"{at}: fewer than {s['minItems']} items")
    if "maxItems" in s and len(data) > s["maxItems"]:
        errors.append(f"{at}: more than {s['maxItems']} items")
    prefix = s.get("prefixItems", [])
    for i, item in enumerate(data):
        item_schema = prefix[i] if i < len(prefix) else s.get("items")
        if item_schema is not None:
            errors += _check(item_schema, item, root, f"{at}[{i}]")
    return errors


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- replay doubles
class EnvReplay:
    """A core.Env replaying one recorded game: `reset` returns the recorded start, and each
    `step` must get the recorded actions (`recorded_actions`), returning the recorded result."""

    def __init__(self, recording: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.teams: dict[str, TeamSpec] = decode(dict[str, TeamSpec], recording["teams"])
        self.board: Literal["grid", "tank"] = recording["board"]
        self.seed: int = recording["seed"]
        self._start = decode(dict[str, np.ndarray], recording["reset"]["obs"])
        self._info = decode(dict[str, float], recording["reset"]["info"])
        self._frames = [decode(np.ndarray, f) for f in recording["frames"]]
        self.recorded_actions: list[dict[str, np.ndarray]] = [
            decode(dict[str, np.ndarray], s["actions"]) for s in recording["steps"]
        ]
        self._results: list[StepResult] = [
            decode(StepResult, s["result"]) for s in recording["steps"]
        ]
        self.reset_seeds: list[int] = []
        self._t = -1

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> tuple[dict[str, np.ndarray], dict[str, float]]:
        self.reset_seeds.append(seed)
        self._t = 0
        return dict(self._start), dict(self._info)

    def step(self, actions: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> StepResult:
        if self._t < 0 or self._t >= len(self._results):
            raise RuntimeError("step called before reset or after the game is over")
        expected = self.recorded_actions[self._t]
        if set(actions) != set(expected):
            raise ValueError(f"teams {sorted(actions)} differ from the recording's {sorted(expected)}")
        for team, action in expected.items():
            if not np.array_equal(np.asarray(actions[team]), action):
                raise ValueError(f"step {self._t}: {team} actions differ from the recording")
        result = self._results[self._t]
        self._t += 1
        return result

    def frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return self._frames[max(self._t, 0)]


class AgentReplay:
    """A core.Agent replaying recorded calls in order: each `act` must get the recorded
    observation and `explore`, and returns the recorded actions; each `learn` must get the
    recorded Transition. `calls` lists what it was asked."""

    def __init__(self, recording: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.team: TeamSpec = decode(TeamSpec, recording["team"])
        self._calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = recording["calls"]
        self._next = 0
        self.calls: list[str] = []

    def _expect(self, method: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if self._next >= len(self._calls):
            raise RuntimeError(f"{method} called after the {len(self._calls)} recorded calls")
        call = self._calls[self._next]
        if call["method"] != method:
            raise ValueError(f"call {self._next}: {method}, but the recording has {call['method']}")
        self._next += 1
        self.calls.append(method)
        return call

    def act(self, obs: np.ndarray, *, explore: bool) -> np.ndarray:
        call = self._expect("act")
        if not np.array_equal(obs, decode(np.ndarray, call["obs"])) or explore != call["explore"]:
            raise ValueError(f"act {self._next - 1}: observation or explore differ")
        return decode(np.ndarray, call["returns"])

    def learn(self, t: Transition) -> None:
        call = self._expect("learn")
        recorded = decode(Transition, call["transition"])
        same = all(
            np.array_equal(getattr(t, f.name), getattr(recorded, f.name))
            for f in dataclasses.fields(Transition)
        )
        if not same:
            raise ValueError(f"learn {self._next - 1}: the transition differs from the recording")

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        path.write_text(json.dumps({"next": self._next}), encoding="utf-8")
        self.calls.append("save")

    def load(self, path: Path) -> None:
        self._next = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["next"]
        self.calls.append("load")


class RunWriterReplay:
    """A runs.RunWriter that keeps everything in memory and builds the RunRecord on `finish`.
    `calls` lists the calls it got, as the runs.RunWriter sample records them."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path, settings: Any) -> None:
        self.path = path
        self.settings = settings
        self.points: list[CurvePoint] = []
        self.checkpoints: dict[str, Path] = {}
        self.generations: dict[str, list[Path]] = {}
        self.games: tuple[GameRecording, ...] = ()
        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def add_point(self, p: CurvePoint) -> None:
        self.points.append(p)
        self.calls.append({"method": "add_point", "step": p.step})

    def save_checkpoint(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str = "agents") -> None:
        self.checkpoints[team] = self.path / f"checkpoint-{team}"
        self.calls.append({"method": "save_checkpoint", "team": team})

    def save_generation(self, agent: Agent, *, team: str, index: int) -> Path:
        path = self.path / "generations" / team / str(index)
        self.generations.setdefault(team, []).append(path)
        self.calls.append({"method": "save_generation", "team": team, "index": index})
        return path

    def save_games(self, games: Sequence[GameRecording]) -> None:
        self.games = tuple(games)
        self.calls.append({"method": "save_games", "n": len(self.games)})

    def finish(self, status: Literal["complete", "incomplete"]) -> RunRecord:
        self.calls.append({"method": "finish", "status": status})
        return RunRecord(
            path=self.path,
            settings=self.settings,
            curve=tuple(self.points),
            status=status,
            checkpoints=dict(self.checkpoints),
            generations={team: tuple(paths) for team, paths in self.generations.items()},
            games=self.games,
        )


class ReplayViewerDouble:
    """A viz.Replay that draws nothing: `advance` moves one frame on, stopping at the last."""

    def __init__(self, game: GameRecording, *, fps: float = 8.0) -> None:
        self.game = game
        self.fps = fps
        self.index = 0
        self.shown = False

    def advance(self) -> int:
        self.index = min(self.index + 1, self.game.frames.shape[0] - 1)
        return self.index

    def show(self) -> None:
        while self.index < self.game.frames.shape[0] - 1:
            self.advance()
        self.shown = True


def env_double(name: str, case: str) -> EnvReplay:
    """The Env replay double of recorded game `case` in folder `name`."""
    return EnvReplay(raw_sample(name, case))


def agent_double(name: str, case: str) -> AgentReplay:
    """The Agent replay double of recording `case` in folder `name`."""
    return AgentReplay(raw_sample(name, case))

81	def decode(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
82	    """`data` (parsed JSON) as an instance of the type `tp`; dataclass defaults fill gaps."""
83	    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
84	    if tp is Any:
85	        return data
86	    if tp is np.ndarray:
87	        return np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=data["dtype"]).reshape(data["shape"])
88	    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp) and isinstance(tp, type):
89	        hints = get_type_hints(tp)
90	        return tp(**{key: decode(hints[key], value) for key, value in data.items()})
91	    if origin is Literal:
92	        if data not in args:
93	            raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not one of {args}")
94	        return data
95	    if origin in {Union, types.UnionType}:
96	        if data is None and type(None) in args:
97	            return None
98	        (inner,) = [a for a in args if a is not type(None)]
99	        return decode(inner, data)
100	    if origin is tuple:
101	        if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
102	            return tuple(decode(args[0], x) for x in data)
103	        return tuple(decode(a, x) for a, x in zip(args, data, strict=True))
104	    if origin in {list, Sequence}:
105	        return [decode(args[0], x) for x in data]
106	    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
107	        return {key: decode(args[1], value) for key, value in data.items()}
108	    if tp is Path:
109	        return Path(data)
110	    if tp is float and isinstance(data, int | float) and not isinstance(data, bool):
111	        return float(data)
112	    if tp in {int, str, bool} and type(data) is tp:
113	        return data
114	    raise TypeError(f"cannot decode {data!r} as {tp}")

117	def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
118	    """The JSON form `decode` reads back."""
119	    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
120	        return {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
121	    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
122	        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
123	    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
124	        return value.item()
125	    if isinstance(value, Path):
126	        return value.as_posix()
127	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
128	        return {str(k): encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
129	    if isinstance(value, list | tuple):
130	        return [encode(v) for v in value]
131	    return value

    if origin in {list, Sequence}:
        return [decode(args[0], x) for x in data]
    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
        return {key: decode(args[1], value) for key, value in data.items()}
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(data)
    if tp is float and isinstance(data, int | float) and not isinstance(data, bool):
        return float(data)
    if tp in {int, str, bool} and type(data) is tp:
        return data
    raise TypeError(f"cannot decode {data!r} as {tp}")


def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
    """The JSON form `decode` reads back."""
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        return {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return value.as_posix()
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(k): encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list | tuple):
        return [encode(v) for v in value]
    return value

    """`data` (parsed JSON) as an instance of the type `tp`; dataclass defaults fill gaps."""

        hints = get_type_hints(tp)
        return tp(**{key: decode(hints[key], value) for key, value in data.items()})
    origin = get_origin(tp)
    if origin is not None:
        return _decode_generic(origin, get_args(tp), data)
    return _decode_scalar(tp, data)


def _decode_generic(origin: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any) -> Any:
    if origin is Literal:
        if data not in args:
            raise ValueError(f"{data!r} is not one of {args}")
        return data
    if origin in {Union, types.UnionType}:
        inner = [a for a in args if a is not type(None)]
        return None if data is None and len(inner) < len(args) else decode(inner[0], data)
    if origin is tuple:
        if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
            return tuple(decode(args[0], x) for x in data)
        return tuple(decode(a, x) for a, x in zip(args, data, strict=True))
    if origin in {list, Sequence}:
        return [decode(args[0], x) for x in data]
    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
        return {key: decode(args[1], value) for key, value in data.items()}
    raise TypeError(f"cannot decode {data!r} as {origin}")


def _decode_scalar(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
    if tp is Any or (tp in {int, str, bool} and type(data) is tp):
        return data
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(data)
    if tp is float and isinstance(data, int | float) and not isinstance(data, bool):
        return float(data)
    raise TypeError(f"cannot decode {data!r} as {tp}")

    """The JSON form `decode` reads back."""
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):

        return {str(k): encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list | tuple):
        return [encode(v) for v in value]
    return _encode_scalar(value)


def _encode_scalar(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return value.as_posix()
    return value

I'll write a scratch script to generate samples, schemas, and recordings, simulating Snake, the corridor, the tank, and predator-prey games realistically enough to produce believable recorded data.

"""Tiny simulators that produce realistic recordings for the contract doubles."""

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO, ActionSpace, StepResult, TeamSpec
from rllab.envs import COMPACT_FIELDS

HEADINGS = ["up", "right", "down", "left"]
DELTA = {"up": (-1, 0), "right": (0, 1), "down": (1, 0), "left": (0, -1)}


class SnakeSim:
    def __init__(self, view, foods, size=10, rewards=(1.0, -1.0, 0.0)):
        self.view, self.size, self.foods = view, size, list(foods)
        self.food_r, self.death_r, self.step_r = rewards
        self.body = [(5, 4), (5, 3), (5, 2)]
        self.heading = "right"
        self.food = self.foods.pop(0)
        self.score = 0
        self.steps = 0
        self.since_eat = 0
        self.over = False

    @property
    def team(self):
        shape = (11,) if self.view == "compact" else (3, self.size, self.size)
        return TeamSpec(1, shape, ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))

    def _blocked(self, cell):
        r, c = cell
        if not (0 <= r < self.size and 0 <= c < self.size):
            return True
        return cell in self.body[:-1]

    def _ahead(self, heading):
        dr, dc = DELTA[heading]
        r, c = self.body[0]
        return (r + dr, c + dc)

    def _turn(self, action):
        i = HEADINGS.index(self.heading)
        return HEADINGS[(i + {0: 0, 1: -1, 2: 1}[action]) % 4]

    def obs(self):
        if self.view == "compact":
            values = dict.fromkeys(COMPACT_FIELDS, 0.0)
            values["danger_straight"] = float(self._blocked(self._ahead(self._turn(0))))
            values["danger_left"] = float(self._blocked(self._ahead(self._turn(1))))
            values["danger_right"] = float(self._blocked(self._ahead(self._turn(2))))
            values[f"head_{self.heading}"] = 1.0
            (hr, hc), (fr, fc) = self.body[0], self.food
            values["food_left"] = float(fc < hc)
            values["food_right"] = float(fc > hc)
            values["food_up"] = float(fr < hr)
            values["food_down"] = float(fr > hr)
            return np.array([[values[f] for f in COMPACT_FIELDS]], dtype=np.float32)
        grid = np.zeros((1, 3, self.size, self.size), dtype=np.float32)
        grid[0, 0][self.body[0]] = 1
        for cell in self.body[1:]:
            grid[0, 1][cell] = 1
        grid[0, 2][self.food] = 1
        return grid

    def frame(self):
        f = np.zeros((self.size, self.size), dtype=np.int8)
        for cell in self.body[1:]:
            f[cell] = 2
        f[self.body[0]] = 1
        f[self.food] = 3
        return f

    def info(self):
        return {"steps": float(self.steps), f"score:{SOLO}": float(self.score), "score": float(self.score)}

    def step(self, action):
        self.heading = self._turn(action)
        head = self._ahead(self.heading)
        self.steps += 1
        terminated = truncated = done = False
        reward = self.step_r
        eats = head == self.food
        if self._blocked(head) and not (eats is False and head == self.body[-1]):
            terminated = done = True
            reward = self.death_r
        else:
            self.body.insert(0, head)
            if eats:
                self.score += 1
                reward = self.food_r
                self.since_eat = 0
                self.food = self.foods.pop(0)
            else:
                self.body.pop()
                self.since_eat += 1
                if self.since_eat >= 100 * len(self.body):
                    truncated = True
        self.over = terminated or truncated
        return StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: np.array([reward], dtype=np.float32)},
            done={SOLO: np.array([done])},
            terminated=terminated,
            truncated=truncated,
            info=self.info(),
        )


class CorridorSim:
    def __init__(self, length=10, max_steps=50):
        self.length, self.max_steps = length, max_steps
        self.pos = 0
        self.steps = 0
        self.score = 0

    team = TeamSpec(1, (10,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=2))

    def obs(self):
        o = np.zeros((1, self.length), dtype=np.float32)
        o[0, self.pos] = 1
        return o

    def frame(self):
        f = np.zeros((1, self.length), dtype=np.int8)
        f[0, self.length - 1] = 3
        f[0, self.pos] = 1
        return f

    def info(self):
        return {"steps": float(self.steps), f"score:{SOLO}": float(self.score), "score": float(self.score)}

    def step(self, action):
        self.pos = min(max(self.pos + (1 if action == 1 else -1), 0), self.length - 1)
        self.steps += 1
        goal = self.pos == self.length - 1
        if goal:
            self.score = 1
        truncated = not goal and self.steps >= self.max_steps
        return StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: np.array([1.0 if goal else 0.0], dtype=np.float32)},
            done={SOLO: np.array([goal])},
            terminated=goal,
            truncated=truncated,
            info=self.info(),
        )


def _clip_len(v):
    n = np.linalg.norm(v, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    return np.where(n > 1, v / np.maximum(n, 1e-9), v)


def _stop_at_wall(p, radius):
    n = np.linalg.norm(p, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    limit = 1 - radius
    return np.where(n > limit, p / np.maximum(n, 1e-9) * limit, p)


class TankSim:
    """FISH-01-FISH-09 roughly: ball reflects, fish swim, catches within 0.11."""

    def __init__(self, rng, n_fish=8):
        self.n = n_fish
        self.ball = np.array([0.1, -0.2])
        angle = 0.6
        self.ball_v = 0.03 * np.array([np.cos(angle), np.sin(angle)])
        fish = []
        # two fish waiting on the ball's path, the rest scattered at least 0.4 away
        for k in (16, 24):
            fish.append(self.ball + self.ball_v * k + np.array([0.01, -0.01]))
        while len(fish) < n_fish:
            p = rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.9, 2)
            if np.linalg.norm(p) < 0.95 and np.linalg.norm(p - self.ball) >= 0.4:
                fish.append(p)
        self.fish = np.array(fish)
        self.fish_v = np.zeros_like(self.fish)
        self.caught = np.zeros(n_fish, dtype=bool)
        self.survived = np.zeros(n_fish)
        self.steps = 0

    team = TeamSpec(8, (8,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2))

    def obs(self):
        rel = self.ball - self.fish
        o = np.concatenate(
            [self.fish, self.fish_v, rel, np.repeat(self.ball_v[None], self.n, 0)], axis=1
        )
        return o.astype(np.float32)

    def frame(self):
        rows = [[*self.ball, 0.08, 0]]
        rows += [[*p, 0.03, 2 if c else 1] for p, c in zip(self.fish, self.caught)]
        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)

    def info(self):
        score = float(self.survived.mean())
        return {"steps": float(self.steps), f"score:{SOLO}": score, "score": score}

    def step(self, actions):
        nxt = self.ball + self.ball_v
        if np.linalg.norm(nxt) > 1 - 0.08:
            normal = nxt / np.linalg.norm(nxt)
            self.ball_v = self.ball_v - 2 * np.dot(self.ball_v, normal) * normal
            nxt = self.ball + self.ball_v
        self.ball = nxt
        move = _clip_len(actions.astype(np.float64)) * 0.024
        move[self.caught] = 0
        new = _stop_at_wall(self.fish + move, 0.03)
        self.fish_v = new - self.fish
        self.fish = new
        self.steps += 1
        newly = (~self.caught) & (np.linalg.norm(self.fish - self.ball, axis=1) < 0.11)
        reward = np.where(self.caught | newly, 0.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)
        self.survived += (~self.caught & ~newly).astype(float)
        self.caught |= newly
        self.fish_v[self.caught] = 0
        terminated = bool(self.caught.all())
        return StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: reward},
            done={SOLO: self.caught.copy()},
            terminated=terminated,
            truncated=not terminated and self.steps >= 1000,
            info=self.info(),
        ), int(newly.sum())


class PredPreySim:
    """HUNT-01-HUNT-09 roughly: one predator, six prey, eight pellets."""

    def __init__(self, rng):
        self.rng = rng
        self.pred = np.array([0.0, 0.0])
        self.pred_v = np.zeros(2)
        prey = [np.array([0.45, 0.0])]  # in the predator's way
        pellets = [np.array([-0.5, 0.3]), np.array([0.2, -0.6])]
        prey += [pellets[0] + np.array([0.1, 0.0]), pellets[1] + np.array([0.0, 0.1])]
        while len(prey) < 6:
            p = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, 2)
            if np.linalg.norm(p) < 0.9 and np.linalg.norm(p - self.pred) >= 0.4:
                prey.append(p)
        while len(pellets) < 8:
            p = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, 2)
            if np.linalg.norm(p) < 0.9 and np.linalg.norm(p - self.pred) >= 0.4:
                pellets.append(p)
        self.prey = np.array(prey)
        self.prey_v = np.zeros_like(self.prey)
        self.pellets = np.array(pellets)
        self.pellet_back = np.zeros(8, dtype=int)  # steps until a pellet reappears
        self.out = np.zeros(6, dtype=int)  # steps a caught prey stays out
        self.catches = 0
        self.prey_total = np.zeros(6)
        self.steps = 0

    teams = {
        "predator": TeamSpec(1, (19,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
        "prey": TeamSpec(6, (18,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
    }

    def _pred_obs(self):
        o = [*self.pred, *self.pred_v]
        swimming = np.flatnonzero(self.out == 0)
        dists = np.linalg.norm(self.prey[swimming] - self.pred, axis=1)
        order = swimming[np.argsort(dists)]
        for k in range(3):
            if k < len(order) and np.linalg.norm(self.prey[order[k]] - self.pred) <= 0.6:
                i = order[k]
                o += [1.0, *(self.prey[i] - self.pred), *self.prey_v[i]]
            else:
                o += [0.0] * 5
        return np.array([o], dtype=np.float32)

    def _prey_obs(self):
        rows = []
        live = np.flatnonzero(self.pellet_back == 0)
        for i in range(6):
            o = [*self.prey[i], *self.prey_v[i]]
            rel = self.pred - self.prey[i]
            o += [1.0, *rel, *self.pred_v] if np.linalg.norm(rel) <= 0.5 else [0.0] * 5
            d = np.linalg.norm(self.pellets[live] - self.prey[i], axis=1)
            near = live[np.argsort(d)]
            for k in range(3):
                if k < len(near) and np.linalg.norm(self.pellets[near[k]] - self.prey[i]) <= 0.5:
                    o += [1.0, *(self.pellets[near[k]] - self.prey[i])]
                else:
                    o += [0.0] * 3
            rows.append(o[:18])
        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)

    def obs(self):
        return {"predator": self._pred_obs(), "prey": self._prey_obs()}

    def frame(self):
        rows = [[*self.pred, 0.04, 3]]
        rows += [[*p, 0.03, 5 if o else 4] for p, o in zip(self.prey, self.out)]
        rows += [[*p, 0.02, 6] for p, b in zip(self.pellets, self.pellet_back) if b == 0]
        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)

    def info(self):
        prey_score = float(self.prey_total.mean())
        return {
            "steps": float(self.steps),
            "score:predator": float(self.catches),
            "score:prey": prey_score,
            "score": float(self.catches),
        }

    def step(self, actions):
        pm = _clip_len(actions["predator"][0].astype(np.float64)) * 0.03
        new = _stop_at_wall(self.pred + pm, 0.04)
        self.pred_v, self.pred = new - self.pred, new
        qm = _clip_len(actions["prey"].astype(np.float64)) * 0.024
        qm[self.out > 0] = 0
        newq = _stop_at_wall(self.prey + qm, 0.03)
        self.prey_v, self.prey = newq - self.prey, newq
        self.steps += 1
        pred_r = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float32)
        prey_r = np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float32)
        eaten = 0
        for i in range(6):
            if self.out[i] > 0:
                continue
            if np.linalg.norm(self.prey[i] - self.pred) < 0.08:
                self.out[i] = 50
                self.catches += 1
                pred_r[0] += 1
                prey_r[i] -= 5
                self.prey_v[i] = 0
                continue
            for j in np.flatnonzero(self.pellet_back == 0):
                if np.linalg.norm(self.pellets[j] - self.prey[i]) < 0.05:
                    self.pellet_back[j] = 20
                    prey_r[i] += 1
                    eaten += 1
                    break
        self.pellet_back = np.maximum(self.pellet_back - (self.pellet_back > 0), 0)
        self.prey_total += prey_r
        return StepResult(
            obs=self.obs(),
            reward={"predator": pred_r, "prey": prey_r},
            done={"predator": np.array([False]), "prey": self.out > 0},
            terminated=False,
            truncated=self.steps >= 1000,
            info=self.info(),
        ), int(pred_r[0]), eaten
