wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/tests/contracts/_gen/sims.py
"""Tiny simulators that produce realistic recordings for the contract doubles."""

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import SOLO, ActionSpace, StepResult, TeamSpec
from rllab.envs import COMPACT_FIELDS

HEADINGS = ["up", "right", "down", "left"]
DELTA = {"up": (-1, 0), "right": (0, 1), "down": (1, 0), "left": (0, -1)}


class SnakeSim:
    def __init__(self, view, foods, size=10, rewards=(1.0, -1.0, 0.0)):
        self.view, self.size, self.foods = view, size, list(foods)
        self.food_r, self.death_r, self.step_r = rewards
        self.body = [(5, 4), (5, 3), (5, 2)]
        self.heading = "right"
        self.food = self.foods.pop(0)
        self.score = 0
        self.steps = 0
        self.since_eat = 0

    @property
    def team(self):
        shape = (11,) if self.view == "compact" else (3, self.size, self.size)
        return TeamSpec(1, shape, ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))

    def _blocked(self, cell):
        r, c = cell
        if not (0 <= r < self.size and 0 <= c < self.size):
            return True
        return cell in self.body[:-1]

    def _ahead(self, heading):
        dr, dc = DELTA[heading]
        r, c = self.body[0]
        return (r + dr, c + dc)

    def _turn(self, action):
        i = HEADINGS.index(self.heading)
        return HEADINGS[(i + {0: 0, 1: -1, 2: 1}[action]) % 4]

    def obs(self):
        if self.view == "compact":
            values = dict.fromkeys(COMPACT_FIELDS, 0.0)
            values["danger_straight"] = float(self._blocked(self._ahead(self._turn(0))))
            values["danger_left"] = float(self._blocked(self._ahead(self._turn(1))))
            values["danger_right"] = float(self._blocked(self._ahead(self._turn(2))))
            values[f"head_{self.heading}"] = 1.0
            (hr, hc), (fr, fc) = self.body[0], self.food
            values["food_left"] = float(fc < hc)
            values["food_right"] = float(fc > hc)
            values["food_up"] = float(fr < hr)
            values["food_down"] = float(fr > hr)
            return np.array([[values[f] for f in COMPACT_FIELDS]], dtype=np.float32)
        grid = np.zeros((1, 3, self.size, self.size), dtype=np.float32)
        grid[0, 0][self.body[0]] = 1
        for cell in self.body[1:]:
            grid[0, 1][cell] = 1
        grid[0, 2][self.food] = 1
        return grid

    def frame(self):
        f = np.zeros((self.size, self.size), dtype=np.int8)
        for cell in self.body[1:]:
            f[cell] = 2
        f[self.body[0]] = 1
        f[self.food] = 3
        return f

    def info(self):
        s = float(self.score)
        return {"steps": float(self.steps), f"score:{SOLO}": s, "score": s}

    def step(self, action):
        self.heading = self._turn(action)
        head = self._ahead(self.heading)
        self.steps += 1
        terminated = truncated = done = False
        reward = self.step_r
        eats = head == self.food
        if self._blocked(head):
            terminated = done = True
            reward = self.death_r
        else:
            self.body.insert(0, head)
            if eats:
                self.score += 1
                reward = self.food_r
                self.since_eat = 0
                self.food = self.foods.pop(0)
            else:
                self.body.pop()
                self.since_eat += 1
                truncated = self.since_eat >= 100 * len(self.body)
        return StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: np.array([reward], dtype=np.float32)},
            done={SOLO: np.array([done])},
            terminated=terminated,
            truncated=truncated,
            info=self.info(),
        )


class CorridorSim:
    team = TeamSpec(1, (10,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=2))

    def __init__(self, length=10, max_steps=50):
        self.length, self.max_steps = length, max_steps
        self.pos = 0
        self.steps = 0
        self.score = 0

    def obs(self):
        o = np.zeros((1, self.length), dtype=np.float32)
        o[0, self.pos] = 1
        return o

    def frame(self):
        f = np.zeros((1, self.length), dtype=np.int8)
        f[0, self.length - 1] = 3
        f[0, self.pos] = 1
        return f

    def info(self):
        s = float(self.score)
        return {"steps": float(self.steps), f"score:{SOLO}": s, "score": s}

    def step(self, action):
        self.pos = min(max(self.pos + (1 if action == 1 else -1), 0), self.length - 1)
        self.steps += 1
        goal = self.pos == self.length - 1
        if goal:
            self.score = 1
        truncated = not goal and self.steps >= self.max_steps
        return StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: np.array([1.0 if goal else 0.0], dtype=np.float32)},
            done={SOLO: np.array([goal])},
            terminated=goal,
            truncated=truncated,
            info=self.info(),
        )


def clip_len(v):
    n = np.linalg.norm(v, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    return np.where(n > 1, v / np.maximum(n, 1e-9), v)


def stop_at_wall(p, radius):
    n = np.linalg.norm(p, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    limit = 1 - radius
    return np.where(n > limit, p / np.maximum(n, 1e-9) * limit, p)


class TankSim:
    """FISH-01-FISH-09 roughly: the ball reflects, fish swim, catches within 0.11."""

    team = TeamSpec(8, (8,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2))

    def __init__(self, rng, n_fish=8):
        self.n = n_fish
        self.ball = np.array([0.1, -0.2])
        angle = 0.6
        self.ball_v = 0.03 * np.array([np.cos(angle), np.sin(angle)])
        fish = [self.ball + self.ball_v * k + np.array([0.01, -0.01]) for k in (16, 24)]
        while len(fish) < n_fish:
            p = rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.9, 2)
            if np.linalg.norm(p) < 0.95 and np.linalg.norm(p - self.ball) >= 0.4:
                fish.append(p)
        self.fish = np.array(fish)
        self.fish_v = np.zeros_like(self.fish)
        self.caught = np.zeros(n_fish, dtype=bool)
        self.survived = np.zeros(n_fish)
        self.steps = 0

    def obs(self):
        rel = self.ball - self.fish
        ball_v = np.repeat(self.ball_v[None], self.n, 0)
        return np.concatenate([self.fish, self.fish_v, rel, ball_v], axis=1).astype(np.float32)

    def frame(self):
        rows = [[*self.ball, 0.08, 0]]
        rows += [[*p, 0.03, 2 if c else 1] for p, c in zip(self.fish, self.caught, strict=True)]
        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)

    def info(self):
        s = float(self.survived.mean())
        return {"steps": float(self.steps), f"score:{SOLO}": s, "score": s}

    def step(self, actions):
        nxt = self.ball + self.ball_v
        if np.linalg.norm(nxt) > 1 - 0.08:
            normal = nxt / np.linalg.norm(nxt)
            self.ball_v = self.ball_v - 2 * np.dot(self.ball_v, normal) * normal
            nxt = self.ball + self.ball_v
        self.ball = nxt
        move = clip_len(actions.astype(np.float64)) * 0.024
        move[self.caught] = 0
        new = stop_at_wall(self.fish + move, 0.03)
        self.fish_v = new - self.fish
        self.fish = new
        self.steps += 1
        newly = (~self.caught) & (np.linalg.norm(self.fish - self.ball, axis=1) < 0.11)
        reward = np.where(self.caught | newly, 0.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)
        self.survived += (~self.caught & ~newly).astype(float)
        self.caught |= newly
        self.fish_v[self.caught] = 0
        terminated = bool(self.caught.all())
        result = StepResult(
            obs={SOLO: self.obs()},
            reward={SOLO: reward},
            done={SOLO: self.caught.copy()},
            terminated=terminated,
            truncated=not terminated and self.steps >= 1000,
            info=self.info(),
        )
        return result, int(newly.sum())


class PredPreySim:
    """HUNT-01-HUNT-09 roughly: one predator, six prey, eight pellets."""

    teams = {
        "predator": TeamSpec(1, (19,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
        "prey": TeamSpec(6, (18,), ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2)),
    }

    def __init__(self, rng):
        self.pred = np.array([0.0, 0.0])
        self.pred_v = np.zeros(2)
        pellets = [np.array([-0.5, 0.3]), np.array([0.2, -0.6])]
        prey = [np.array([0.45, 0.0])]
        prey += [pellets[0] + np.array([0.1, 0.0]), pellets[1] + np.array([0.0, 0.1])]
        while len(prey) < 6:
            p = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, 2)
            if np.linalg.norm(p) < 0.9 and np.linalg.norm(p - self.pred) >= 0.4:
                prey.append(p)
        while len(pellets) < 8:
            p = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85, 2)
            if np.linalg.norm(p) < 0.9 and np.linalg.norm(p - self.pred) >= 0.4:
                pellets.append(p)
        self.prey = np.array(prey)
        self.prey_v = np.zeros_like(self.prey)
        self.pellets = np.array(pellets)
        self.pellet_back = np.zeros(8, dtype=int)
        self.out = np.zeros(6, dtype=int)
        self.catches = 0
        self.prey_total = np.zeros(6)
        self.steps = 0

    def _pred_obs(self):
        o = [*self.pred, *self.pred_v]
        swimming = np.flatnonzero(self.out == 0)
        dists = np.linalg.norm(self.prey[swimming] - self.pred, axis=1)
        order = swimming[np.argsort(dists)]
        for k in range(3):
            if k < len(order) and np.linalg.norm(self.prey[order[k]] - self.pred) <= 0.6:
                i = order[k]
                o += [1.0, *(self.prey[i] - self.pred), *self.prey_v[i]]
            else:
                o += [0.0] * 5
        return np.array([o], dtype=np.float32)

    def _prey_obs(self):
        rows = []
        live = np.flatnonzero(self.pellet_back == 0)
        for i in range(6):
            o = [*self.prey[i], *self.prey_v[i]]
            rel = self.pred - self.prey[i]
            o += [1.0, *rel, *self.pred_v] if np.linalg.norm(rel) <= 0.5 else [0.0] * 5
            d = np.linalg.norm(self.pellets[live] - self.prey[i], axis=1)
            near = live[np.argsort(d)]
            for k in range(3):
                if k < len(near) and np.linalg.norm(self.pellets[near[k]] - self.prey[i]) <= 0.5:
                    o += [1.0, *(self.pellets[near[k]] - self.prey[i])]
                else:
                    o += [0.0] * 3
            rows.append(o[:18])
        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)

    def obs(self):
        return {"predator": self._pred_obs(), "prey": self._prey_obs()}

    def frame(self):
        rows = [[*self.pred, 0.04, 3]]
        rows += [[*p, 0.03, 5 if o else 4] for p, o in zip(self.prey, self.out, strict=True)]
        live = zip(self.pellets, self.pellet_back, strict=True)
        rows += [[*p, 0.02, 6] for p, b in live if b == 0]
        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)

    def info(self):
        return {
            "steps": float(self.steps),
            "score:predator": float(self.catches),
            "score:prey": float(self.prey_total.mean()),
            "score": float(self.catches),
        }

    def step(self, actions):
        pm = clip_len(actions["predator"][0].astype(np.float64)) * 0.03
        new = stop_at_wall(self.pred + pm, 0.04)
        self.pred_v, self.pred = new - self.pred, new
        qm = clip_len(actions["prey"].astype(np.float64)) * 0.024
        qm[self.out > 0] = 0
        newq = stop_at_wall(self.prey + qm, 0.03)
        self.prey_v, self.prey = newq - self.prey, newq
        self.steps += 1
        self.out = np.maximum(self.out - 1, 0) * (self.out > 1)
        pred_r = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float32)
        prey_r = np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float32)
        eaten = 0
        for i in range(6):
            if self.out[i] > 0:
                continue
            if np.linalg.norm(self.prey[i] - self.pred) < 0.08:
                self.out[i] = 50
                self.catches += 1
                pred_r[0] += 1
                prey_r[i] -= 5
                self.prey_v[i] = 0
                continue
            for j in np.flatnonzero(self.pellet_back == 0):
                if np.linalg.norm(self.pellets[j] - self.prey[i]) < 0.05:
                    self.pellet_back[j] = 21
                    prey_r[i] += 1
                    eaten += 1
                    break
        self.pellet_back = np.maximum(self.pellet_back - 1, 0)
        self.prey_total += prey_r
        result = StepResult(
            obs=self.obs(),
            reward={"predator": pred_r, "prey": prey_r},
            done={"predator": np.array([False]), "prey": self.out > 0},
            terminated=False,
            truncated=self.steps >= 1000,
            info=self.info(),
        )
        return result, int(pred_r[0]), eaten

 else:\n                    o += [0.0] * 3\n            rows.append(o[:18])\n        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    def obs(self):\n        return {\"predator\": self._pred_obs(), \"prey\": self._prey_obs()}\n\n    def frame(self):\n        rows = [[*self.pred, 0.04, 3]]\n        rows += [[*p, 0.03, 5 if o else 4] for p, o in zip(self.prey, self.out, strict=True)]\n        live = zip(self.pellets, self.pellet_back, strict=True)\n        rows += [[*p, 0.02, 6] for p, b in live if b == 0]\n        return np.array(rows, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    def info(self):\n        return {\n            \"steps\": float(self.steps),\n            \"score:predator\": float(self.catches),\n            \"score:prey\": float(self.prey_total.mean()),\n            \"score\": float(self.catches),\n        }\n\n    def step(self, actions):\n        pm = clip_len(actions[\"predator\"][0].astype(np.float64)) * 0.03\n        new = stop_at_wall(self.pred + pm, 0.04)\n        self.pred_v, self.pred = new - self.pred, new\n        qm = clip_len(actions[\"prey\"].astype(np.float64)) * 0.024\n        qm[self.out > 0] = 0\n        newq = stop_at_wall(self.prey + qm, 0.03)\n        self.prey_v, self.prey = newq - self.prey, newq\n        self.steps += 1\n        self.out = np.maximum(self.out - 1, 0) * (self.out > 1)\n        pred_r = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float32)\n        prey_r = np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float32)\n        eaten = 0\n        for i in range(6):\n            if self.out[i] > 0:\n                continue\n            if np.linalg.norm(self.prey[i] - self.pred) < 0.08:\n                self.out[i] = 50\n                self.catches += 1\n                pred_r[0] += 1\n                prey_r[i] -= 5\n                self.prey_v[i] = 0\n                continue\n            for j in np.flatnonzero(self.pellet_back == 0):\n                if np.linalg.norm(self.pellets[j] - self.prey[i]) < 0.05:\n                    self.pellet_back[j] = 21\n                    prey_r[i] += 1\n                    eaten += 1\n                    break\n        self.pellet_back = np.maximum(self.pellet_back - 1, 0)\n        self.prey_total += prey_r\n        result = StepResult(\n            obs=self.obs(),\n            reward={\"predator\": pred_r, \"prey\": prey_r},\n            done={\"predator\": np.array([False]), \"prey\": self.out > 0},\n            terminated=False,\n            truncated=self.steps >= 1000,\n            info=self.info(),\n        )\n        return result, int(pred_r[0]), eaten\n"
}

"""Generate every contract folder's schema.json and samples (run once, then deleted)."""

import dataclasses
import importlib.util
import json
import sys
import tomllib
import types as pytypes
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Union, get_args, get_origin, get_type_hints

import numpy as np

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("contract_kit", HERE.parent / "kit.py")
kit = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
sys.modules["contract_kit"] = kit
spec.loader.exec_module(kit)

import sims  # noqa: E402

from rllab.compare import Claim, ClaimSpec  # noqa: E402
from rllab.core import (  # noqa: E402
    SOLO,
    ActionSpace,
    AgentParams,
    Exploration,
    Rewards,
    RunSettings,
    StepResult,
    TeamSpec,
    Transition,

from rllab.envs import COMPACT_FIELDS  # noqa: E402

enc = kit.encode

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ schemas
NDARRAY = {
    "type": "object",
    "required": ["dtype", "shape", "data"],
    "properties": {
        "dtype": {"enum": ["float32", "float64", "int64", "int8", "bool"]},
        "shape": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0}},
        "data": {"type": ["array", "number", "integer", "boolean"]},
    },
    "additionalProperties": False,
}
LIMITS = {
    (TeamSpec, "n_agents"): {"minimum": 1},
    (ActionSpace, "n"): {"minimum": 0},
    (ActionSpace, "dim"): {"minimum": 0},
    (Exploration, "start"): {"minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
    (Exploration, "end"): {"minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
    (AgentParams, "gamma"): {"minimum": 0, "maximum": 1},
    (RunSettings, "seed"): {"minimum": 0, "maximum": 999_999},
    (RunSettings, "steps"): {"minimum": 1},
    (RunSettings, "n_agents"): {"minimum": 1, "maximum": 16},
    (EvalOptions, "n_games"): {"minimum": 1, "maximum": 1000},
    (EvalOptions, "record"): {"minimum": 0},
    (CurvePoint, "step"): {"minimum": 0},
    (CurvePoint, "games"): {"minimum": 0},
}


def schema_for(tp, defs):
    origin, args = get_origin(tp), get_args(tp)
    if tp is np.ndarray:
        defs["ndarray"] = NDARRAY
        return {"$ref": "#/$defs/ndarray"}
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        name = tp.__name__
        if name not in defs:
            defs[name] = {}
            hints = get_type_hints(tp)
            props, required = {}, []
            for f in dataclasses.fields(tp):
                props[f.name] = {**schema_for(hints[f.name], defs), **LIMITS.get((tp, f.name), {})}
                if f.default is dataclasses.MISSING and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING:
                    required.append(f.name)
            defs[name] = {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": props,
                "required": required,
                "additionalProperties": False,
            }
        return {"$ref": f"#/$defs/{name}"}
    if origin is Literal:
        return {"enum": list(args)}
    if origin in {Union, pytypes.UnionType}:
        return {"anyOf": [schema_for(a, defs) for a in args]}
    if origin is tuple:
        if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
            return {"type": "array", "items": schema_for(args[0], defs)}
        return {
            "type": "array",
            "prefixItems": [schema_for(a, defs) for a in args],
            "minItems": len(args),
            "maxItems": len(args),
        }
    if origin in {list, Sequence}:
        return {"type": "array", "items": schema_for(args[0], defs)}
    if origin in {dict, Mapping}:
        return {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": schema_for(args[1], defs)}
    return {
        str: {"type": "string"},
        Path: {"type": "string"},
        int: {"type": "integer"},
        float: {"type": "number"},
        bool: {"type": "boolean"},
        type(None): {"type": "null"},
    }[tp]


def document(title, body, defs):
    return {
        "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
        "title": title,
        "$defs": defs,
        **body,
    }


def type_schema(title, tp):
    defs = {}
    ref = schema_for(tp, defs)
    return document(title, ref, defs)


def obj(props, defs, required=None):
    return {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": props,
        "required": list(props) if required is None else required,
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }


def write(name, schema, samples, extra_files=None):
    folder = OUT / name
    (folder / "samples").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (folder / "schema.json").write_text(json.dumps(schema, indent=1) + "\n")
    for case, data in samples.items():
        errors = kit.validate(schema, data)
        assert not errors, (name, case, errors[:5])
        (folder / "samples" / f"{case}.json").write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=1) + "\n")
    for rel, text in (extra_files or {}).items():
        (folder / rel).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (folder / rel).write_text(text)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ games
def record_env(sim, actions, seed, teams, board):
    reset_obs = sim.obs() if isinstance(sim.obs(), dict) else {SOLO: sim.obs()}
    reset = {"obs": enc(reset_obs), "info": sim.info()}
    frames = [sim.frame()]
    steps, results = [], []
    for a in actions:
        out = sim.step(a if not isinstance(a, dict) else a)
        result = out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out
        acts = a if isinstance(a, dict) else {SOLO: np.asarray(a)}
        if not isinstance(a, dict) and np.ndim(a) == 0:
            acts = {SOLO: np.array([a], dtype=np.int64)}
        steps.append({"actions": enc(acts), "result": enc(result)})
        results.append(out)
        frames.append(sim.frame())
    rec = {
        "board": board,
        "seed": seed,
        "teams": enc(teams),
        "reset": reset,
        "frames": enc(frames),
        "steps": steps,
    }
    return rec, results, frames


def game_of(rec, frames, seed):
    teams = list(rec["teams"])
    rewards = []
    scores = {t: [rec["reset"]["info"][f"score:{t}"]] for t in teams}
    for s in rec["steps"]:
        r = kit.decode(StepResult, s["result"])
        rewards.append(sum(float(v.sum()) for v in r.reward.values()))
        for t in teams:
            scores[t].append(r.info[f"score:{t}"])
    dtype = np.int8 if rec["board"] == "grid" else np.float32
    if rec["board"] == "tank":
        k = max(f.shape[0] for f in frames)
        frames = [np.vstack([f, np.zeros((k - f.shape[0], 4), np.float32)]) for f in frames]
    return GameRecording(
        seed=seed,
        board=rec["board"],
        frames=np.array(frames, dtype=dtype),
        rewards=np.array(rewards, dtype=np.float32),
        scores={t: np.array(v, dtype=np.float32) for t, v in scores.items()},
    )


def snake_wall(view="compact"):
    sim = sims.SnakeSim(view, foods=[(8, 6), (2, 2), (7, 7)])
    actions = [0] * 5 + [1] + [0] * 3 + [1] + [0] * 8 + [2, 0]
    rec, results, frames = record_env(sim, actions, 7, {SOLO: sim.team}, "grid")
    last = kit.decode(StepResult, rec["steps"][-1]["result"])
    assert len(actions) == 20 and last.terminated and last.reward[SOLO][0] == -1.0
    assert not any(kit.decode(StepResult, s["result"]).terminated for s in rec["steps"][:-1])
    return rec, frames


def snake_self(view="grid"):
    sim = sims.SnakeSim(view, foods=[(5, 6), (5, 8), (8, 1)])
    actions = [0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 2, 2, 2]
    rec, results, frames = record_env(sim, actions, 11, {SOLO: sim.team}, "grid")
    res = [kit.decode(StepResult, s["result"]) for s in rec["steps"]]
    assert res[-1].terminated and res[-1].info["score"] == 2.0
    assert [r.reward[SOLO][0] for r in res].count(1.0) == 2
    assert not any(r.terminated for r in res[:-1])
    return rec, frames


def corridor(actions, seed):
    sim = sims.CorridorSim()
    rec, results, frames = record_env(sim, actions, seed, {SOLO: sim.team}, "grid")
    return rec, frames


def tank_game():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    sim = sims.TankSim(rng)
    acts = []
    catches = 0
    probe_rng = np.random.default_rng(4)
    for _ in range(30):
        a = probe_rng.uniform(-1, 1, (8, 2)).astype(np.float32)
        a[:2] = 0
        acts.append({SOLO: a})
    rec, results, frames = record_env(sim, acts, 21, {SOLO: sims.TankSim.team}, "tank")
    catches = sum(n for _, n in results)
    assert catches == 2, catches
    return rec, frames


def predprey_game():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(5)
    sim = sims.PredPreySim(rng)
    acts = []
    probe = np.random.default_rng(6)
    for _ in range(40):
        pred = np.array([[1.0, 0.0]], dtype=np.float32)
        prey = probe.uniform(-0.3, 0.3, (6, 2)).astype(np.float32)
        prey[0] = 0
        prey[1] = [-1.0, 0.0]
        prey[2] = [0.0, -1.0]
        acts.append({"predator": pred, "prey": prey})
    rec, results, frames = record_env(sim, acts, 33, sims.PredPreySim.teams, "tank")
    catches = sum(c for _, c, _ in results)
    eaten = sum(e for _, _, e in results)
    assert catches == 1 and eaten == 2, (catches, eaten)
    return rec, frames


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ settings
DQN_PARAMS = AgentParams(lr=5e-4, gamma=0.99)
TAB_PARAMS = AgentParams(lr=0.1, gamma=0.9)

def settings(**kw):
    base = {
        "name": "dqn-compact",
        "env": "snake",
        "view": "compact",
        "agent": "dqn",
        "n_agents": 1,
        "opponents": {},
        "self_play": "none",
        "pool_latest": 0.5,
        "generations": 0,
        "ablation": "none",
        "rewards": Rewards(),
        "exploration": Exploration(),
        "params": DQN_PARAMS,
        "steps": 200_000,
        "seed": 0,
        "eval_every": 10_000,
        "eval_games": 50,
    }
    return RunSettings(**{**base, **kw})

def toml_of(data):
    lines, tables = [], []
    for key, value in data.items():
        if isinstance(value, dict) and value:
            tables.append((key, value))
        elif isinstance(value, dict):
            lines.append(f"{key} = {{}}")
        else:
            lines.append(f"{key} = {json.dumps(value)}")
    for key, value in tables:
        lines.append(f"\n[{key}]")
        lines += [f"{k} = {json.dumps(v)}" for k, v in value.items()]
    text = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    assert tomllib.loads(text) == data, data
    return text


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ main
def main():
    wall, wall_frames = snake_wall()
    self_hit, self_frames = snake_self()
    goal, goal_frames = corridor([1] * 9, 0)
    capped, _ = corridor([1, 0] * 25, 4)
    wander, wander_frames = corridor([0, 0] + [1] * 9, 2)
    tank, tank_frames = tank_game()
    hunt, hunt_frames = predprey_game()

    # --- core: plain types
    write("core.SOLO", document("core.SOLO", {"const": "agents"}, {}), {"value": SOLO})
    teams = {
        "snake_compact": sims.SnakeSim("compact", [(0, 0)]).team,
        "tank_school": sims.TankSim.team,
        "predprey_predator": sims.PredPreySim.teams["predator"],
        "predprey_prey": sims.PredPreySim.teams["prey"],
    }
    write("core.TeamSpec", type_schema("core.TeamSpec", TeamSpec), enc(teams))
    write(
        "core.ActionSpace",
        type_schema("core.ActionSpace", ActionSpace),
        enc(
            {
                "snake_discrete": ActionSpace("discrete", n=3),
                "tank_continuous": ActionSpace("continuous", dim=2),
            }
        ),
    )
    write(
        "core.Rewards",
        type_schema("core.Rewards", Rewards),
        enc({"defaults": Rewards(), "food_ten": Rewards(food=10.0), "tank_survival": TANK_REWARDS}),
    )
    write(
        "core.Exploration",
        type_schema("core.Exploration", Exploration),
        enc({"defaults": Exploration(), "no_decay": Exploration(start=0.1, end=0.1)}),
    )
    write(
        "core.AgentParams",
        type_schema("core.AgentParams", AgentParams),
        enc({"dqn_compact": DQN_PARAMS, "tabular": TAB_PARAMS, "ppo": PPO_PARAMS}),
    )
    run_settings = enc(SETTINGS)
    write(
        "core.RunSettings",
        type_schema("core.RunSettings", RunSettings),
        run_settings,
        {f"samples/{k}.toml": toml_of(v) for k, v in run_settings.items()},
    )

    # --- StepResult and Transition
    def step_of(rec, i):
        return rec["steps"][i]["result"]

    w = wall["steps"]
    eat_i = next(
        i for i, s in enumerate(self_hit["steps"]) if s["result"]["reward"][SOLO]["data"][0] == 1.0
    )
    tank_catch_i = next(
        i
        for i, s in enumerate(tank["steps"])
        if sum(s["result"]["done"][SOLO]["data"])
        > (sum(tank["steps"][i - 1]["result"]["done"][SOLO]["data"]) if i else 0)
    )
    hunt_catch_i = next(
        i for i, s in enumerate(hunt["steps"]) if s["result"]["reward"]["predator"]["data"][0] == 1
    )
    starve_obs = kit.decode(StepResult, w[3]["result"]).obs
    starving = StepResult(
        obs=starve_obs,
        reward={SOLO: np.array([0.0], np.float32)},
        done={SOLO: np.array([False])},
        terminated=False,
        truncated=True,
        info={"steps": 1012.0, f"score:{SOLO}": 7.0, "score": 7.0},
    )
    t_rng = np.random.default_rng(9)
    fish_obs = t_rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8, (8, 8)).astype(np.float32)
    caught = np.array([1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0], dtype=bool)
    capped_tank = StepResult(
        obs={SOLO: fish_obs},
        reward={SOLO: np.where(caught, 0.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)},
        done={SOLO: caught},
        terminated=False,
        truncated=True,
        info={"steps": 1000.0, f"score:{SOLO}": 702.375, "score": 702.375},
    )
    write(
        "core.StepResult",
        type_schema("core.StepResult", StepResult),
        {
            "ordinary": step_of(wall, 0),
            "food": step_of(self_hit, eat_i),
            "wall_collision": step_of(wall, 19),
            "starvation_truncation": enc(starving),
            "tank_fish_caught": step_of(tank, tank_catch_i),
            "tank_step_cap": enc(capped_tank),
            "predprey_catch": step_of(hunt, hunt_catch_i),
        },
    )

    def transition(rec, i, team=SOLO, prev_obs=None):
        s = kit.decode(StepResult, rec["steps"][i]["result"])
        if i == 0:
            obs = kit.decode(dict[str, np.ndarray], rec["reset"]["obs"])[team]
        else:
            obs = kit.decode(StepResult, rec["steps"][i - 1]["result"]).obs[team]
        acts = kit.decode(dict[str, np.ndarray], rec["steps"][i]["actions"])[team]
        before = (
            np.zeros(len(acts), bool)
            if i == 0
            else kit.decode(StepResult, rec["steps"][i - 1]["result"]).done[team]
        )
        return Transition(
            obs=obs,
            actions=acts,
            reward=s.reward[team],
            next_obs=s.obs[team],
            terminated=s.done[team] & ~before,
            active=~before,
            truncated=s.truncated,
        )

    starve_t = Transition(
        obs=kit.decode(StepResult, w[2]["result"]).obs[SOLO],
        actions=np.array([0], np.int64),
        reward=np.array([0.0], np.float32),
        next_obs=starve_obs[SOLO],
        terminated=np.array([False]),
        active=np.array([True]),
        truncated=True,
    )
    tank_t = transition(tank, tank_catch_i)
    assert tank_t.active.all() and tank_t.terminated.sum() == 1, (tank_t.active, tank_t.terminated)
    write(
        "core.Transition",
        type_schema("core.Transition", Transition),
        enc(
            {
                "food": transition(self_hit, eat_i),
                "collision": transition(wall, 19),
                "truncated": starve_t,
                "tank_one_caught": tank_t,
            }
        ),
    )

    # --- Env doubles
    rec_defs = {}
    rec_schema = obj(
        {
            "board": {"enum": ["grid", "tank"]},
            "seed": {"type": "integer"},
            "teams": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": schema_for(TeamSpec, rec_defs)},
            "reset": obj(
                {
                    "obs": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": schema_for(np.ndarray, rec_defs)},
                    "info": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": {"type": "number"}},
                },
                rec_defs,
            ),
            "frames": {"type": "array", "items": schema_for(np.ndarray, rec_defs), "minItems": 1},
            "steps": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": obj(
                    {
                        "actions": {
                            "type": "object",
                            "additionalProperties": schema_for(np.ndarray, rec_defs),
                        },
                        "result": schema_for(StepResult, rec_defs),
                    },
                    rec_defs,
                ),
            },
        },
        rec_defs,
    )
    env_schema = document("EnvRecording: a recorded game an EnvReplay double replays", rec_schema, rec_defs)
    write(
        "core.Env",
        env_schema,
        {"snake_wall": wall, "corridor_goal": goal, "tank_two_catches": tank, "predprey_catch": hunt},
    )
    write("envs.Snake", env_schema, {"compact_wall": wall, "grid_self_collision": self_hit})
    write("envs.Corridor", env_schema, {"goal": goal, "truncated": capped})
    write(
        "envs.COMPACT_FIELDS",
        document("envs.COMPACT_FIELDS", {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "minItems": 11, "maxItems": 11}, {}),
        {"fields": list(COMPACT_FIELDS)},
    )

    # --- Agent doubles
    a_defs = {}
    act_call = obj(
        {
            "method": {"const": "act"},
            "obs": schema_for(np.ndarray, a_defs),
            "explore": {"type": "boolean"},
            "returns": schema_for(np.ndarray, a_defs),
        },
        a_defs,
    )
    learn_call = obj(
        {"method": {"const": "learn"}, "transition": schema_for(Transition, a_defs)}, a_defs
    )
    agent_schema = document(
        "AgentRecording: recorded calls an AgentReplay double replays",
        obj(
            {
                "team": schema_for(TeamSpec, a_defs),
                "calls": {"type": "array", "items": {"anyOf": [act_call, learn_call]}},
            },
            a_defs,
        ),
        a_defs,
    )

    def act_learn(rec, n, explore=True):
        calls = []
        for i in range(n):
            t = transition(rec, i)
            calls.append({"method": "act", "obs": enc(t.obs), "explore": explore, "returns": enc(t.actions)})
            calls.append({"method": "learn", "transition": enc(t)})
        return calls

    def acts_only(obs_list, returns, explore_list):
        return [
            {"method": "act", "obs": enc(o), "explore": e, "returns": enc(np.array([r], np.int64))}
            for o, r, e in zip(obs_list, returns, explore_list, strict=True)
        ]

    corridor_team = enc(sims.CorridorSim.team)
    compact_team = enc(teams["snake_compact"])
    tabular = {"team": corridor_team, "calls": act_learn(goal, 5)}
    write("core.Agent", agent_schema, {"tabular_corridor": tabular})
    write("agents.TabularAgent", agent_schema, {"corridor": tabular})
    write("agents.DQNAgent", agent_schema, {"compact_after_learn_start": {"team": compact_team, "calls": act_learn(wall, 5)}})
    wall_obs = [kit.decode(dict[str, np.ndarray], wall["reset"]["obs"])[SOLO]] + [
        kit.decode(StepResult, s["result"]).obs[SOLO] for s in wall["steps"][:-1]
    ]
    rand = np.random.default_rng(0).integers(0, 3, 10)
    write(
        "baselines.RandomPlayer",
        agent_schema,
        {"seeded_ten": {"team": compact_team, "calls": acts_only(wall_obs[:10], rand, [i % 2 == 0 for i in range(10)])}},
    )

    def compact(**on):
        values = dict.fromkeys(COMPACT_FIELDS, 0.0)
        values.update(dict.fromkeys(on, 1.0))
        return np.array([[values[f] for f in COMPACT_FIELDS]], np.float32)

    write(
        "baselines.ScriptedPlayer",
        agent_schema,
        {
            "food_ahead_safe": {
                "team": compact_team,
                "calls": acts_only([compact(head_right=1, food_right=1)], [0], [False]),
            },
            "food_left_danger_left": {
                "team": compact_team,
                "calls": acts_only([compact(head_right=1, food_up=1, danger_left=1)], [0], [False]),
            },
        },
    )
    write(
        "reference.ReferencePlayer",
        agent_schema,
        {"greedy_five": {"team": compact_team, "calls": acts_only(wall_obs[:5], [0, 0, 0, 0, 0], [False] * 5)}},
    )

    # --- runs
    corridor_game = game_of(goal, goal_frames, 1_000_000)
    games5 = tuple(dataclasses.replace(corridor_game, seed=1_000_000 + i) for i in range(5))
    curve3 = (
        CurvePoint(step=0, games=0, mean_score=0.05, elapsed_s=0.4),
        CurvePoint(step=2_500, games=171, mean_score=0.6, elapsed_s=3.1),
        CurvePoint(step=5_000, games=402, mean_score=1.0, elapsed_s=6.2),

    corridor_path = Path("runs/corridor-tabular-s0-20261018-101500")

    write(

    write(

        type_schema("runs.GameRecording", GameRecording),

    # RunWriter replay double: the calls train makes, and the record finish returns

    # --- compare and viz
    specs = {
        "greater_than_random": ClaimSpec("dqn-compact greater than random", "dqn-compact", "random", "greater"),
        "not_worse_than_reference": ClaimSpec(
            "dqn-compact not worse than reference", "dqn-compact", "reference", "not_worse", min_ratio=0.9
        ),
        "stretch_grid": ClaimSpec("dqn-grid greater than scripted", "dqn-grid", "scripted", "greater", stretch=True),
    }
    method = "one-sided Welch t-test on per-seed means (5 v 5); Holm correction over 3 claims"
    claims = {
        "passed_greater": Claim(specs["greater_than_random"], 18.4, 0.31, 0.0004, 0.0012, True, method),
        "failed_not_worse": Claim(specs["not_worse_than_reference"], 14.2, 17.6, 0.011, 0.022, False, method),

    def curve_run(name, seed):
        base = settings_for(name)
        path = Path(f"runs/{name}-s{seed}-20261018-0{seed}0000")
        return RunRecord(
            path=path,
            settings=dataclasses.replace(base, seed=seed),
            curve=(
                CurvePoint(0, 0, round(0.1 + 0.02 * seed, 3), 2.0),
                CurvePoint(base.steps // 2, 300 + 10 * seed, round(6.0 + seed * 0.4, 3), 600.0),
                CurvePoint(base.steps, 700 + 10 * seed, round(12.0 + seed * 0.5, 3), 1200.0),
            ),
            status="complete",
            checkpoints={SOLO: path / ("checkpoint.npz" if base.agent == "tabular" else "checkpoint.pt")},
            generations={},
            games=(),
        )

    def settings_for(name):
        if name.startswith("tabular"):
            s = settings(name=name, agent="tabular", params=TAB_PARAMS)
            return dataclasses.replace(s, ablation="no_explore") if name.endswith("no-explore") else s
        if name == "reference":
            return dataclasses.replace(REFERENCE_SNAKE, name=name)
        view = "grid" if name == "dqn-grid" else "compact"
        ablation = {"dqn-compact-no-memory": "no_memory", "dqn-compact-no-target": "no_target"}.get(name, "none")
        return settings(name=name, view=view, ablation=ablation)

    curve_runs = CurveRuns(
        ladder={n: [curve_run(n, s) for s in range(5)] for n in ladder},
        ablations={n: [curve_run(n, s) for s in range(5)] for n in ("dqn-compact", *ablations[:2], "tabular-compact", ablations[2])},
    )
    write("lab.CurveRuns", type_schema("lab.CurveRuns", CurveRuns), {"m1_groups": enc(curve_runs)})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def tank_game():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    sim = sims.TankSim(rng)
    acts = []
    catches = 0
    probe_rng = np.random.default_rng(4)
    for _ in range(30):
        a = probe_rng.uniform(-1, 1, (8, 2)).astype(np.float32)
        a[:2] = 0
        acts.append({SOLO: a})
    rec, results, frames = record_env(sim, acts, 21, {SOLO: sims.TankSim.team}, "tank")
    catches = sum(n for _, n in results)
    assert catches == 2, catches
    return rec, frames
new
def tank_game():
    for seed in range(3, 100):
        sim = sims.TankSim(np.random.default_rng(seed))
        probe_rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000)
        acts = []
        for _ in range(30):
            a = probe_rng.uniform(-1, 1, (8, 2)).astype(np.float32)
            a[:2] = 0
            acts.append({SOLO: a})
        rec, results, frames = record_env(sim, acts, 21, {SOLO: sims.TankSim.team}, "tank")
        if sum(n for _, n in results) == 2:
            return rec, frames
    raise AssertionError("no tank game with exactly 2 catches")

new
SCALAR_LIST = re.compile(r"\[[\s\d.eE+\-,truefals]*\]")


def dumps(data):
    text = json.dumps(data, indent=1)
    return SCALAR_LIST.sub(lambda m: json.dumps(json.loads(m.group(0))), text)

edited /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/tests/contracts/_gen/gen.py · old
def dumps(data):

new
def shorten(obj):
    if isinstance(obj, dict) and obj.get("dtype") == "float32" and "data" in obj:
        arr = np.asarray(obj["data"], dtype=np.float32)
        short = [float(str(x)) for x in arr.ravel()]
        back = np.asarray(short, dtype=np.float32).reshape(arr.shape)
        assert np.array_equal(back, arr)
        return {**obj, "data": back.astype(np.float64).round(7).tolist() if False else np.asarray(short).reshape(arr.shape).tolist()}
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {k: shorten(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    if isinstance(obj, list):
        return [shorten(v) for v in obj]
    return obj


def dumps(data):
    text = json.dumps(shorten(data), indent=1)

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-incremental-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-af0ddf25de9219b55/tests/contracts/core.load_settings/fake.py
"""The core.load_settings fake: the fixture files under files/ and what each loads to."""

from pathlib import Path

import contract_kit as kit

from rllab.core import RunSettings

NAME = "core.load_settings"
FILES = kit.folder(NAME) / "files"


def load_settings(path: Path) -> RunSettings:
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"settings file {path} not found")
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for case in kit.raw_samples(NAME).values():
        if (FILES / case["file"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8") != text:
            continue
        if "error" in case:
            raise ValueError(f"{case['key']}: refused in {path}")
        return kit.decode(RunSettings, case["settings"])
    raise LookupError(f"no load_settings fixture matches {path}")

"""The runs.create_run fake: writes settings.json and status=running, returns a RunWriterReplay."""

import json
from pathlib import Path

import contract_kit as kit

from rllab.core import RunSettings


def create_run(settings: RunSettings, *, root: Path = Path("runs")) -> kit.RunWriterReplay:
    base = f"{settings.name}-s{settings.seed}"
    taken = {p.name for p in root.glob(f"{base}-*")} if root.is_dir() else set()
    path = root / f"{base}-{len(taken):04d}"
    path.mkdir(parents=True)
    (path / "settings.json").write_text(json.dumps(kit.encode(settings)), encoding="utf-8")
    (path / "status").write_text("running", encoding="utf-8")
    return kit.RunWriterReplay(path, settings)

"""The runs.open_run fake: the fixture run folders by name; any other folder is missing."""

import dataclasses
from pathlib import Path

import contract_kit as kit

from rllab.runs import RunRecord

NAME = "runs.open_run"


def open_run(path: Path) -> RunRecord:
    for case in kit.raw_samples(NAME).values():
        if Path(case["path"]).name == path.name and "record" in case:
            return dataclasses.replace(kit.decode(RunRecord, case["record"]), path=path)
    raise FileNotFoundError(f"run {path}: no settings.json")

import contract_kit as kit

NAME = "evaluate.eval_seeds"


def eval_seeds(train_seed: int, n: int) -> list[int]:
    for case in kit.raw_samples(NAME).values():
        if case["train_seed"] == train_seed and n <= case["n"]:
            return list(case["seeds"][:n])
    raise LookupError(f"no eval_seeds fixture for train seed {train_seed}, n={n}")

"""The evaluate.evaluate fake: the scripted-player ScoreSet for seed 0, cut to the options.

It never builds an environment or calls an agent; `calls` records each call's arguments."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from typing import Any

import contract_kit as kit

from rllab.core import Agent, Env
from rllab.evaluate import EvalOptions, ScoreSet

NAME = "evaluate.evaluate"
calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []


def evaluate(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    *,
    player: str,
    train_seed: int,
    options: EvalOptions = EvalOptions(),  # noqa: B008  # the contract's default
) -> ScoreSet:
    calls.append({"player": player, "train_seed": train_seed, "options": options, "teams": list(agents)})
    fixture: ScoreSet = kit.decode(ScoreSet, kit.raw_sample(NAME, "scripted_seed0")["scores"])
    return ScoreSet(
        player=player,
        train_seed=train_seed,
        scores=fixture.scores[: options.n_games],
        games=fixture.games[: options.record],
    )

"""The evaluate.evaluate fake: the scripted-player ScoreSet for seed 0, cut to the options.

It never builds an environment or calls an agent; `calls` records each call's arguments."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from typing import Any

import contract_kit as kit

from rllab.core import Agent, Env
from rllab.evaluate import EvalOptions, ScoreSet

NAME = "evaluate.evaluate"
_DEFAULT_OPTIONS = EvalOptions()
calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []


def evaluate(
    make_env: Callable[[], Env],
    agents: Mapping[str, Agent],
    *,
    player: str,
    train_seed: int,
    options: EvalOptions = _DEFAULT_OPTIONS,
) -> ScoreSet:
    calls.append(
        {"player": player, "train_seed": train_seed, "options": options, "teams": list(agents)}
    )
    fixture: ScoreSet = kit.decode(ScoreSet, kit.raw_sample(NAME, "scripted_seed0")["scores"])
    return ScoreSet(
        player=player,
        train_seed=train_seed,
        scores=fixture.scores[: options.n_games],
        games=fixture.games[: options.record],
    )

"""The compare.run_claims fake: one passed and one failed Claim, and a report.md listing them."""

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path

import contract_kit as kit

from rllab.compare import Claim, ClaimSpec
from rllab.evaluate import ScoreSet

NAME = "compare.run_claims"


def run_claims(
    scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
    specs: Sequence[ClaimSpec],
    *,
    alpha: float = 0.05,
    charts: Sequence[Path] = (),
    out: Path,
) -> list[Claim]:
    missing = sorted({name for s in specs for name in (s.a, s.b)} - set(scores))
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"claims name players missing from the scores: {missing}")
    claims: list[Claim] = kit.decode(
        list[Claim], kit.raw_sample(NAME, "one_passed_one_failed")["claims"]
    )
    lines = [f"- {c.spec.name}: {'passed' if c.passed else 'failed'}" for c in claims]
    lines += [f"![cross-play]({chart.as_posix()})" for chart in charts]
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out / "report.md").write_text("\n".join(["# Report", *lines]) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return claims

from rllab.runs import RunRecord

    "iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mNk+M9QDwADhgGAWjR9awAAAABJRU5ErkJggg=="

def plot_curves(
    groups: Mapping[str, Sequence[RunRecord]],
    *,
    scores: Mapping[str, Sequence[ScoreSet]],
    baselines: Sequence[str],
    out: Path,
) -> Chart:
    missing = sorted(set(baselines) - set(scores))
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"baselines missing from the scores: {missing}")
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_bytes(_PNG)
    chart: Chart = kit.decode(Chart, kit.raw_sample(NAME, "ladder")["chart"])
    return dataclasses.replace(chart, path=out)

"""Every contract sample validates against its schema and its type; the replay doubles replay."""

import dataclasses
import importlib.util
import sys
import tomllib
from pathlib import Path
from types import ModuleType
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.compare import Claim, ClaimSpec
from rllab.core import (
    SOLO,
    ActionSpace,
    AgentParams,
    Exploration,
    Rewards,
    RunSettings,
    StepResult,
    TeamSpec,
    Transition,
)
from rllab.envs import COMPACT_FIELDS
from rllab.evaluate import EvalOptions, ScoreSet
from rllab.lab import CurveRuns, PlayerTarget, ReportTargets
from rllab.runs import CurvePoint, GameRecording, RunRecord
from rllab.viz import Chart


def _load_kit() -> ModuleType:
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("contract_kit", Path(__file__).with_name("kit.py"))
    assert spec is not None
    assert spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules["contract_kit"] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


kit = _load_kit()

TYPES: dict[str, Any] = {
    "core.TeamSpec": TeamSpec,
    "core.ActionSpace": ActionSpace,
    "core.StepResult": StepResult,
    "core.Transition": Transition,
    "core.RunSettings": RunSettings,
    "core.Rewards": Rewards,
    "core.Exploration": Exploration,
    "core.AgentParams": AgentParams,
    "runs.CurvePoint": CurvePoint,
    "runs.GameRecording": GameRecording,
    "runs.RunRecord": RunRecord,
    "evaluate.ScoreSet": ScoreSet,
    "evaluate.EvalOptions": EvalOptions,
    "compare.ClaimSpec": ClaimSpec,
    "compare.Claim": Claim,
    "viz.Chart": Chart,
    "lab.PlayerTarget": PlayerTarget,
    "lab.ReportTargets": ReportTargets,
    "lab.CurveRuns": CurveRuns,
}
CONSTANTS = {"core.SOLO": SOLO, "envs.COMPACT_FIELDS": list(COMPACT_FIELDS)}
ENV_DOUBLES = ("core.Env", "envs.Snake", "envs.Corridor")
AGENT_DOUBLES = (
    "core.Agent",
    "agents.TabularAgent",
    "agents.DQNAgent",
    "baselines.RandomPlayer",
    "baselines.ScriptedPlayer",
    "reference.ReferencePlayer",
)
OTHER_DOUBLES = ("runs.RunWriter", "viz.Replay")
FAKES = (
    "core.load_settings",
    "runs.create_run",
    "runs.open_run",
    "evaluate.eval_seeds",
    "evaluate.evaluate",
    "train.train",
    "reference.train_reference",
    "compare.run_claims",
    "viz.plot_curves",
)
FOLDERS = sorted({*TYPES, *CONSTANTS, *ENV_DOUBLES, *AGENT_DOUBLES, *OTHER_DOUBLES, *FAKES})
CASES = [(name, case) for name in FOLDERS for case in kit.raw_samples(name)]


def test_every_contract_folder_is_known() -> None:
    on_disk = {p.name for p in kit.CONTRACTS.iterdir() if p.is_dir() and "." in p.name}
    assert on_disk == set(FOLDERS)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", FOLDERS)
def test_folder_has_schema_and_samples(name: str) -> None:
    assert kit.schema(name)["title"]
    assert kit.raw_samples(name), f"{name} has no samples"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "case"), CASES)
def test_sample_validates_against_its_schema(name: str, case: str) -> None:
    assert kit.validate(kit.schema(name), kit.raw_sample(name, case)) == []


def test_schema_rejects_a_wrong_sample() -> None:
    bad = {**kit.raw_sample("core.RunSettings", "dqn_compact"), "env": "chess"}
    assert kit.validate(kit.schema("core.RunSettings"), bad)
    missing = {k: v for k, v in kit.raw_sample("core.Rewards", "defaults").items() if k != "food"}
    assert kit.validate(kit.schema("core.Rewards"), missing) == []  # food has a default
    assert kit.validate(kit.schema("core.TeamSpec"), {"n_agents": 1})


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "case"), [(n, c) for n, c in CASES if n in TYPES])
def test_type_sample_decodes_and_round_trips(name: str, case: str) -> None:
    value = kit.sample(name, case, TYPES[name])
    assert isinstance(value, TYPES[name])
    again = kit.decode(TYPES[name], kit.encode(value))
    assert kit.encode(again) == kit.encode(value)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", sorted(CONSTANTS))
def test_constant_matches_its_sample(name: str) -> None:
    (only,) = kit.raw_samples(name).values()
    assert only == CONSTANTS[name]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", sorted(kit.raw_samples("core.RunSettings")))
def test_run_settings_toml_is_the_sample(case: str) -> None:
    toml = kit.folder("core.RunSettings") / "samples" / f"{case}.toml"
    assert tomllib.loads(toml.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) == kit.raw_sample("core.RunSettings", case)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "change",
    [
        {"view": "grid", "env": "corridor"},
        {"seed": 1_000_000},
        {"steps": 0},
        {"agent": "ppo"},
        {"ablation": "no_explore"},
        {"n_agents": 2},
        {"opponents": {"predator": "learn"}},
    ],
)
def test_run_settings_refuse_broken_invariants(change: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    good = kit.sample("core.RunSettings", "dqn_compact", RunSettings)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"\S"):
        dataclasses.replace(good, **change)


def test_type_invariants_refuse_bad_values() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="discrete"):
        ActionSpace("discrete", n=1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="continuous"):
        ActionSpace("continuous", n=3)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="n_agents"):
        TeamSpec(0, (11,), ActionSpace("discrete", n=3))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="exploration"):
        Exploration(start=0.1, end=0.5)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="gamma"):
        AgentParams(lr=0.1, gamma=0.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="n_games"):
        EvalOptions(n_games=1001)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="exactly one"):
        PlayerTarget()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- env doubles
ENV_CASES = [(n, c) for n in ENV_DOUBLES for c in kit.raw_samples(n)]


def _check_batch(team: TeamSpec, obs: np.ndarray) -> None:
    assert obs.dtype == np.float32
    assert obs.shape == (team.n_agents, *team.obs_shape)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "case"), ENV_CASES)
def test_env_double_replays_its_recording(name: str, case: str) -> None:
    env = kit.env_double(name, case)
    obs, info = env.reset(env.seed)
    first = next(iter(env.teams))
    assert set(obs) == set(env.teams)
    assert {"steps", "score", f"score:{first}"} <= set(info)
    for team, spec in env.teams.items():
        _check_batch(spec, obs[team])
    for actions in env.recorded_actions:
        result = env.step(actions)
        for team, spec in env.teams.items():
            _check_batch(spec, result.obs[team])
            assert result.reward[team].shape == (spec.n_agents,)
            assert result.reward[team].dtype == np.float32
            assert result.done[team].dtype == np.bool_
            assert result.info[f"score:{team}"] == result.info[f"score:{team}"]
        assert result.info["score"] == result.info[f"score:{first}"]
    frame = env.frame()
    assert frame.dtype == (np.int8 if env.board == "grid" else np.float32)
    assert frame.ndim == 2
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        env.step(env.recorded_actions[-1])


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "case"), ENV_CASES)
def test_env_double_actions_follow_the_action_space(name: str, case: str) -> None:
    env = kit.env_double(name, case)
    for actions in env.recorded_actions:
        for team, spec in env.teams.items():
            if spec.actions.kind == "discrete":
                assert actions[team].dtype == np.int64
                assert actions[team].shape == (spec.n_agents,)
                assert actions[team].max() < spec.actions.n
            else:
                assert actions[team].dtype == np.float32
                assert actions[team].shape == (spec.n_agents, spec.actions.dim)


def test_env_double_refuses_other_actions() -> None:
    env = kit.env_double("core.Env", "corridor_goal")
    env.reset(0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="differ"):
        env.step({SOLO: np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)})


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("name", "case", "steps", "ending"),
    [
        ("core.Env", "snake_wall", 20, "terminated"),
        ("core.Env", "corridor_goal", 9, "terminated"),
        ("envs.Corridor", "truncated", 50, "truncated"),
        ("envs.Snake", "grid_self_collision", 12, "terminated"),
    ],
)
def test_recorded_game_ends_as_the_contract_case_says(
    name: str, case: str, steps: int, ending: str
) -> None:
    env = kit.env_double(name, case)
    env.reset(env.seed)
    results = [env.step(a) for a in env.recorded_actions]
    assert len(results) == steps
    assert getattr(results[-1], ending)
    assert not any(r.terminated or r.truncated for r in results[:-1])


def test_tank_and_predprey_recordings_hold_their_events() -> None:
    tank = kit.env_double("core.Env", "tank_two_catches")
    tank.reset(tank.seed)
    last = [tank.step(a) for a in tank.recorded_actions][-1]
    assert len(tank.recorded_actions) == 30
    assert int(last.done[SOLO].sum()) == 2
    hunt = kit.env_double("core.Env", "predprey_catch")
    hunt.reset(hunt.seed)
    results = [hunt.step(a) for a in hunt.recorded_actions]
    assert len(results) == 40
    assert results[-1].info["score:predator"] == 1.0
    assert sum(int((r.reward["prey"] == 1.0).sum()) for r in results) == 2


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------- agent doubles
AGENT_CASES = [(n, c) for n in AGENT_DOUBLES for c in kit.raw_samples(n)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "case"), AGENT_CASES)
def test_agent_double_replays_its_calls(name: str, case: str, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    recording = kit.raw_sample(name, case)
    agent = kit.agent_double(name, case)
    for call in recording["calls"]:
        if call["method"] == "act":
            obs = kit.decode(np.ndarray, call["obs"])
            action = agent.act(obs, explore=call["explore"])
            assert action.shape[0] == agent.team.n_agents
            assert np.array_equal(action, kit.decode(np.ndarray, call["returns"]))
        else:
            agent.learn(kit.decode(Transition, call["transition"]))
    assert agent.calls == [c["method"] for c in recording["calls"]]
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        agent.act(np.zeros((1, *agent.team.obs_shape), np.float32), explore=False)
    agent.save(tmp_path / "replay.json")
    fresh = kit.agent_double(name, case)
    fresh.load(tmp_path / "replay.json")
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        fresh.act(np.zeros((1, *agent.team.obs_shape), np.float32), explore=False)


def test_agent_double_refuses_another_observation() -> None:
    agent = kit.agent_double("core.Agent", "tabular_corridor")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="differ"):
        agent.act(np.zeros((1, 10), np.float32), explore=True)


def test_tabular_recording_follows_the_corridor_game() -> None:
    env = kit.env_double("core.Env", "corridor_goal")
    agent = kit.agent_double("core.Agent", "tabular_corridor")
    obs, _ = env.reset(env.seed)
    for _ in range(5):
        action = agent.act(obs[SOLO], explore=True)
        result = env.step({SOLO: action})
        agent.learn(
            Transition(
                obs=obs[SOLO],
                actions=action,
                reward=result.reward[SOLO],
                next_obs=result.obs[SOLO],
                terminated=result.done[SOLO],
                active=np.ones(1, dtype=bool),
                truncated=result.truncated,
            )
        )
        obs = result.obs
    assert agent.calls == ["act", "learn"] * 5


# -------------------------------------------------------------------------- other doubles
def test_run_writer_double_replays_a_three_point_run() -> None:
    recording = kit.raw_sample("runs.RunWriter", "three_point_complete")
    settings = kit.decode(RunSettings, recording["settings"])
    writer = kit.RunWriterReplay(Path(recording["path"]), settings)
    record = None
    for call in recording["calls"]:
        match call["method"]:
            case "add_point":
                writer.add_point(kit.decode(CurvePoint, call["point"]))
            case "save_checkpoint":
                writer.save_checkpoint(kit.agent_double("core.Agent", "tabular_corridor"), team=call["team"])
            case "save_games":
                writer.save_games(kit.decode(list[GameRecording], call["games"]))
            case "finish":
                record = writer.finish(call["status"])
    assert record is not None
    assert kit.encode(record) == kit.encode(kit.decode(RunRecord, recording["record"]))
    assert [c["method"] for c in writer.calls] == [c["method"] for c in recording["calls"]]


def test_replay_viewer_double_advances_to_the_last_frame() -> None:
    recording = kit.raw_sample("viz.Replay", "twelve_frames")
    game = kit.decode(GameRecording, recording["game"])
    viewer = kit.ReplayViewerDouble(game, fps=recording["fps"])
    assert [viewer.advance() for _ in recording["advances"]] == recording["advances"]
    viewer.show()
    assert viewer.shown
    assert viewer.index == game.frames.shape[0] - 1

"""The frozen interface: signatures, exports, the function fakes and the command line."""

import importlib
import importlib.util
import inspect
import json

def _load_kit() -> ModuleType:
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("contract_kit", Path(__file__).with_name("kit.py"))
    assert spec is not None
    assert spec.loader is not None
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules["contract_kit"] = module
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


kit = _load_kit()

OPTIONS = "EvalOptions(team='agents', n_games=200, record=0)"

EXPORTS = {
    "core": {
        "SOLO", "ActionSpace", "Agent", "AgentParams", "Env", "Exploration", "Rewards",
        "RunSettings", "StepResult", "TeamSpec", "Transition", "load_settings",
    },
    "envs": {"COMPACT_FIELDS", "Corridor", "Snake"},
    "baselines": {"RandomPlayer", "ScriptedPlayer"},

def _shape(fn: Callable[..., Any]) -> str:
    parts, starred = [], False
    for p in inspect.signature(fn).parameters.values():
        if p.name == "self":
            continue
        if p.kind is p.KEYWORD_ONLY and not starred:
            parts.append("*")
            starred = True
        if p.default is p.empty:
            parts.append(p.name)
        else:
            shown = str(p.default) if isinstance(p.default, Path) else repr(p.default)
            parts.append(f"{p.name}={shown}")
    return ", ".join(parts)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("fn", "params"), SIGNATURES, ids=lambda x: getattr(x, "__qualname__", ""))
def test_signature_is_the_contracts(fn: Callable[..., Any], params: str) -> None:
    assert _shape(fn) == params


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("fn", "returns"), RETURNS, ids=lambda x: getattr(x, "__qualname__", ""))
def test_return_type_is_the_contracts(fn: Callable[..., Any], returns: Any) -> None:
    assert get_type_hints(fn)["return"] == returns


@pytest.mark.parametrize("component", sorted(EXPORTS))
def test_component_exports_exactly_its_contract(component: str) -> None:
    module = importlib.import_module(f"rllab.{component}")
    assert set(module.__all__) == EXPORTS[component]
    assert all(hasattr(module, name) for name in module.__all__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- fakes
def test_load_settings_fake_serves_its_files() -> None:
    fake = kit.fake("core.load_settings")
    files = kit.folder("core.load_settings") / "files"
    loaded = fake.load_settings(files / "dqn_compact.toml")
    assert loaded == kit.sample("core.RunSettings", "dqn_compact", RunSettings)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="agent"):
        fake.load_settings(files / "unknown_agent.toml")
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        fake.load_settings(files / "missing.toml")


def test_create_run_fake_writes_a_fresh_folder(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    fixture = kit.raw_sample("runs.create_run", "fresh_corridor")
    settings = kit.decode(RunSettings, fixture["settings"])
    fake = kit.fake("runs.create_run")
    first = fake.create_run(settings, root=tmp_path)
    second = fake.create_run(settings, root=tmp_path)
    assert first.path != second.path
    written = json.loads((first.path / "settings.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert written == fixture["files"]["settings.json"]
    assert (first.path / "status").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == fixture["files"]["status"]


def test_open_run_fake_serves_its_run_and_refuses_others() -> None:
    fake = kit.fake("runs.open_run")
    fixture = kit.raw_sample("runs.open_run", "complete_corridor")
    record = fake.open_run(Path(fixture["path"]))
    assert record.status == "complete"
    assert len(record.curve) == 3
    missing = Path(kit.raw_sample("runs.open_run", "missing_settings")["path"])
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match=missing.name):
        fake.open_run(missing)


def test_eval_seeds_fake_serves_its_seeds() -> None:
    fixture = kit.raw_sample("evaluate.eval_seeds", "seed0_n200")
    seeds = kit.fake("evaluate.eval_seeds").eval_seeds(0, 200)
    assert seeds == fixture["seeds"]
    assert len(set(seeds)) == 200


def test_evaluate_fake_serves_the_scripted_scores() -> None:
    fake = kit.fake("evaluate.evaluate")
    double = kit.agent_double("core.Agent", "tabular_corridor")
    scores = fake.evaluate(lambda: kit.env_double("core.Env", "corridor_goal"), {SOLO: double}, player="p", train_seed=3)
    assert scores.player == "p"
    assert scores.train_seed == 3
    assert scores.scores.shape == (200,)
    assert scores.games == ()
    assert double.calls == []
    short = fake.evaluate(lambda: kit.env_double("core.Env", "corridor_goal"), {SOLO: double}, player="p", train_seed=0, options=EvalOptions(n_games=10, record=1))
    assert short.scores.shape == (10,)
    assert len(short.games) == 1
    assert fake.calls[-1]["options"] == EvalOptions(n_games=10, record=1)


def test_train_fake_serves_a_complete_three_point_run(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    settings = kit.sample("core.RunSettings", "corridor_tabular", RunSettings)
    record = kit.fake("train.train").train(lambda: kit.env_double("core.Env", "corridor_goal"), {}, settings, root=tmp_path)
    assert record.status == "complete"
    assert len(record.curve) == 3
    assert record.settings == settings
    assert record.path.parent == tmp_path
    assert SOLO in record.checkpoints


def test_train_reference_fake_serves_its_run_and_refuses_other_agents(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    fake = kit.fake("reference.train_reference")
    reference = kit.raw_sample("reference.train_reference", "complete_reference")["record"]["settings"]
    record = fake.train_reference(kit.decode(RunSettings, reference), root=tmp_path)
    assert record.status == "complete"
    assert len(record.curve) == 3
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="reference"):
        fake.train_reference(kit.sample("core.RunSettings", "dqn_compact", RunSettings), root=tmp_path)


def _scores(*players: str) -> dict[str, list[ScoreSet]]:
    one = kit.sample("evaluate.ScoreSet", "scripted_seed0", ScoreSet)
    return {p: [one] for p in players}


def test_run_claims_fake_serves_one_passed_one_failed(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    fake = kit.fake("compare.run_claims")
    specs = [kit.sample("compare.ClaimSpec", c, ClaimSpec) for c in ("greater_than_random", "not_worse_than_reference")]
    claims = fake.run_claims(_scores("dqn-compact", "random", "reference"), specs, out=tmp_path)
    assert [c.passed for c in claims] == [True, False]
    assert (tmp_path / "report.md").is_file()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="reference"):
        fake.run_claims(_scores("dqn-compact", "random"), specs, out=tmp_path)


def test_plot_curves_fake_writes_its_chart(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    fake = kit.fake("viz.plot_curves")
    groups: Mapping[str, list[RunRecord]] = {}
    chart = fake.plot_curves(groups, scores=_scores("random", "scripted"), baselines=["random", "scripted"], out=tmp_path / "ladder.png")
    assert chart.path.read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG")
    assert chart.baselines == ("random", "scripted")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="scripted"):
        fake.plot_curves(groups, scores=_scores("random"), baselines=["scripted"], out=tmp_path / "x.png")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- the command line
@pytest.fixture
def entries(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> list[tuple[Any, ...]]:
    calls: list[tuple[Any, ...]] = []
    agent = {"value": "dqn"}

    def settings_with_agent(path: Path) -> Any:
        return type("Settings", (), {"agent": agent["value"]})()

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "load_settings", settings_with_agent)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_train", lambda path, seed: calls.append(("train", path, seed)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_train_reference", lambda path, seed: calls.append(("reference", path, seed)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_evaluate", lambda target, seed, **kw: calls.append(("eval", target, seed, kw)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_report", lambda spec, out: calls.append(("report", spec, out)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_replay", lambda *a: calls.append(("replay", *a)))
    calls.append(agent)
    return calls


def test_main_trains_once_per_seed(entries: list[Any]) -> None:
    assert lab.main(["train", "settings/a.toml", "--seeds", "0-4"]) == 0
    assert entries[1:] == [("train", Path("settings/a.toml"), s) for s in range(5)]


def test_main_sends_reference_settings_to_pl02(entries: list[Any]) -> None:
    entries[0]["value"] = "reference"
    assert lab.main(["train", "settings/r.toml", "--seeds", "3"]) == 0
    assert entries[1:] == [("reference", Path("settings/r.toml"), 3)]


def test_main_evaluates_once_per_target(entries: list[Any]) -> None:
    assert lab.main(["eval", "random", "scripted", "--seed", "2", "--smoke"]) == 0
    kw = {"env": "snake", "team": SOLO, "vs": "scripted"}
    assert entries[1:] == [("eval", "random", 2, kw), ("eval", "scripted", 2, kw)]


def test_main_reports_and_replays(entries: list[Any]) -> None:
    assert lab.main(["report", "reports/smoke.toml"]) == 0
    assert lab.main(["replay", "runs/r", "--new", "--seed", "5"]) == 0
    assert lab.main(["replay", "runs/r", "--pair", "2", "7"]) == 0
    assert entries[1:] == [
        ("report", Path("reports/smoke.toml"), Path("reports/smoke")),
        ("replay", Path("runs/r"), 0, 5, None),
        ("replay", Path("runs/r"), 0, None, (2, 7)),
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("argv", [[], ["bogus"], ["eval"], ["train"], ["replay", "r", "--new", "--pair", "1", "2"]])
def test_main_returns_2_on_bad_arguments(argv: list[str]) -> None:
    assert lab.main(argv) == 2


def test_main_returns_2_on_bad_settings_or_missing_runs(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    def refuse(path: Path) -> Any:
        raise ValueError("agent: unknown 'sarsa'")

    def missing(*args: Any) -> None:
        raise FileNotFoundError("run runs/x: no settings.json")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "load_settings", refuse)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_replay", missing)
    assert lab.main(["train", "settings/bad.toml"]) == 2
    assert lab.main(["replay", "runs/x"]) == 2


def test_numpy_default_arrays_are_float32() -> None:
    assert np.zeros(1, np.float32).dtype == np.float32

@pytest.fixture
def entries(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> SimpleNamespace:
    """The five entry functions and load_settings replaced by recorders of their calls."""
    seen = SimpleNamespace(calls=[], agent="dqn")
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "load_settings", lambda path: SimpleNamespace(agent=seen.agent))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_train", lambda path, seed: seen.calls.append(("train", path, seed)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        cli, "run_train_reference", lambda path, seed: seen.calls.append(("reference", path, seed))
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        cli, "run_evaluate", lambda target, seed, **kw: seen.calls.append(("eval", target, seed, kw))
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_report", lambda spec, out: seen.calls.append(("report", spec, out)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "run_replay", lambda *a: seen.calls.append(("replay", *a)))
    return seen
